{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"path\": \"/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0513_6/projects/faith-evidence-ie/paper_framing.md\", \"content\": \"# Paper Framing — FREIGE\\n\\n> **状态**: 审稿修复中（D103 Action Matrix）— Round 1 得分 3.0 (Findings)，22 个 issue 跟踪中\\n> **最后更新**: 2026-05-23 (D154: RSFT test evidence tax 5/5 完成; Version B \\\"heterogeneous, scale-dependent\\\" 确认)\\n> **审稿 Round 1**: 3.0 (Findings)。3 个系统性问题：claim>evidence、实验混淆、贡献过度\\n> **贡献重构**: 4 贡献 → 3 贡献（CED 合并入 RSFT，EDCR 降级为分析工具）\\n> **Dream v1**: best=2.5, median=3.0, worst=2.5 → reframe 完成\\n> **Dream v2**: best=3.0, median=2.5, worst=2.5\\n\\n## ⚠️ Post-Review Contribution Restructuring (D103)\\n\\n审稿后从 4 贡献重构为 3 贡献：\\n\\n### 原 4 贡献\\n1. Task formalization (evidence-grounded DocRE)\\n2. EDCR diagnostic metric\\n3. Decoupling spectrum\\n4. CED scoring\\n\\n### 新 3 贡献\\n1. **Evidence-grounded DocRE + RSFT with NLI-based scoring** — 合并原 C1+C4。CED 不再作为独立贡献，NLI-based scoring（CED/flat-NLI 等效）是 RSFT 有效的关键组件\\n2. **Decoupling spectrum（观察性，非因果）** — 原 C3 降级为观察性描述。\\\"co-occurs with\\\" 替代 \\\"acts as amplifier\\\"。EDCR 作为分析工具嵌入此贡献（不独立列出）\\n3. **Evidence tax landscape + RSFT variance compression** — 原 C5 提升为正式贡献。Within-family threshold（1.7B 获益，4B+ 中性）+ cross-architecture variation（Qwen 8B ≈0 vs LLaMA 8B evidence helpful）+ RSFT 跨 scale 压缩 seed 方差 (4.6-31×, D118/D152)。**Variance compression 是 C3 核心**（最稳固的 sub-finding，不受 M027 修正影响）\\n\\n### 关键 claim 调整\\n- 所有因果 claim → 观察性描述（GRPO \\\"acts as catastrophic amplifier\\\" → \\\"co-occurs with\\\"）\\n- EDCR ρ 从 -0.965 (n=15) 改为去重后数值（待 Agent 计算完成）\\n- \\\"+8pp\\\" → matched@2048 fair subset: 1.7B=-5.63pp, 4B=+0.73pp, 8B=+0.04pp（D144 corrected）。Abstract 加 \\\"on a controlled evaluation subset\\\"\\n- \\\"matches 8B SFT\\\" → \\\"approaches 8B SFT\\\"\\n- CED vs flat-NLI 不再区分，统称 \\\"NLI-based scoring\\\"\\n\\n## Target Venue\\n\\n- Primary: **EMNLP 2026 Main** (ARR July cycle, ~July 15-25 → commit EMNLP Aug/Sep)\\n- Fallback: AAAI 2027 (abstract July 25, paper Aug 1)\\n\\n## Core Insight\\n\\n**统一主线 (D131)**: Evidence generation in LLM-based DocRE is not a fixed cost — its role depends on **model capacity** (scale-dependent tax), **training paradigm** (decoupling spectrum), and **data quality** (NLI-based filtering). RSFT navigates this landscape by eliminating the tax while preserving evidence faithfulness.\\n\\n本文提出 **evidence-grounded DocRE** 作为新任务范式：LLM 同时输出关系三元组和支撑证据句。核心发现是 evidence generation 的效果并非简单的有益/有害二分，而是一个由三个维度决定的 landscape：\\n\\n1. **Model capacity × evidence tax** (C3): Within-family threshold——1.7B 从 evidence 获益（matched@2048 fair_tax=-5.63pp），4B/8B/14B 趋零（+0.73pp/+0.04pp/+0.03pp）。Cross-architecture variation——LLaMA 8B 与 Qwen 8B pattern 不同（SFT tax 极不稳定，RSFT tax=-2.17pp）。⚠️ D144 修正：4B 从 +7.5pp→+0.73pp，\\\"non-monotonic\\\"/\\\"capacity valley\\\" 叙事已废弃。\\n2. **Training paradigm × decoupling** (C2): RL 优化压力与 evidence-extraction decoupling severity 正相关（GRPO catastrophic > DPO gradual > RSFT none）。越激进的优化越破坏 RE-evidence 平衡。\\n3. **Data quality × training reliability** (C1+C3): NLI-based quality filtering (CED/flat-NLI) 不仅提升 mean performance（+3-5pp），更 dramatically 压缩 seed variance（4.6-31×）。1.7B RSFT（std=0.09pp）比 8B SFT（std=3.23pp）更 predictable——小模型通过 quality filtering 获得大模型才有的稳定性。\\n\\n**RSFT 是唯一同时在三个维度上表现良好的方法**：不引入 decoupling（维度2），压缩 evidence tax 至接近零（维度1），且通过 quality filtering 保证 training reliability（维度3）。\\n\\nCED (Contrastive Evidence Discrimination) 是一种 NLI-based 候选筛选函数，与 flat-NLI 统计不可区分（D078 p=0.44），但均显著优于 random/no scoring。CED 因可解释性优势作为默认推荐。\\n\\n## Story Arc (D131: unified narrative)\\n\\n**One-sentence pitch**: Evidence generation in DocRE is a landscape shaped by capacity, paradigm, and data quality — and RSFT is the only method that navigates all three axes successfully.\\n\\n1. **Problem**: LLM DocRE 无证据 → 部署障碍\\n2. **New task**: evidence-grounded DocRE — 同时生成关系和证据\\n3. **The landscape** (统一主线的三个 facet):\\n   - **3a. Capacity axis** (C3): evidence tax 存在 within-family threshold——1.7B 获益（-5.63pp matched@2048 fair），4B+ 中性（+0.73pp/+0.04pp/+0.03pp）。推翻\\\"evidence 总有代价\\\"。Cross-architecture: Qwen 8B ≈0 vs LLaMA 8B evidence helpful，说明 tax 受架构影响不仅受 scale\\n   - **3b. Paradigm axis** (C2): RL optimization pressure 与 decoupling severity 正相关（GRPO catastrophic > DPO gradual > RSFT none）。QLoRA 放大 mild→catastrophic（intra-task trade-off，非 inter-task forgetting）\\n   - **3c. Quality axis** (C1): NLI-based scoring 不仅提升 mean（+3-5pp），更压缩 variance 4.6-31×（within-scale: 1.7B 31×, 4B 7.5×, 8B 6.5×, LLaMA-8B 4.6×）。Training reliability 是 quality filtering 的本质效应\\n4. **RSFT navigates the landscape**: 唯一同时在三个轴上表现良好的方法——无 decoupling + 压缩 tax + 保证 reliability\\n5. **Practical toolkit**: CED reranker（response-level，恢复 97% evidence 代价），joint generation > post-hoc retrieval（EDCR 低 11pp）\\n6. **Diagnostic**: EDCR 双层诊断——方法级量化 decoupling，per-relation 级定位薄弱关系类型\\n7. **Scaling evidence**: 4 Qwen scales (1.7B/4B/8B/14B) + LLaMA-8B 跨家族 + Re-DocRED 跨数据集\\n8. **Mechanistic decomposition**: duplication regularization (主因, 1.7B 最强) + truncation asymmetry + fair comparison residual — 解释 WHY 而非只报 WHAT。⚠️ 4B 特有分析需 @2048 matched 数据重做（D144 invalidated \\\"capacity valley\\\"）\\n\\n## Core Claims (reframed after Dream v1, updated D068-D083)\\n\\n### Claim 1 (Main): Evidence-grounded DocRE 是可行的新能力\\n- FREIGE 是首个**基于生成式 LLM** 的 evidence-grounded DocRE 方法（⚠️ 必须始终保持 \\\"generative LLM\\\" 限定词——SAIS 已做 encoder-based evidence retrieval + DocRE）\\n- **关键区分 (Reviewer warning)**：encoder-based evidence *retrieval*（SAIS, DREEAM — 分类哪些句子是证据，evidence supervision 提升 RE）vs generative evidence *production*（FREIGE — LLM 生成证据文本，额外生成负担导致 scale-dependent 代价）。论文 Related Work 必须精准讨论此对比，否则审稿人会问 \\\"为什么 SAIS 说 evidence 有用，你说 evidence 有害\\\"\\n- **Evidence generation tax**: scale-dependent，详见 Claim 5（D079/D082/D083）\\n- RSFT 在 4/4 Qwen 配置 + LLaMA 下均超过 SFT baseline（Ign-F1 +1.24~6.55pp）\\n- **CED reranker 是最优实用方案**: rel_f1=0.4864（仅低于 no-evidence 0.29pp），evi_f1=0.8188（plan_005 ✅）\\n- **D076 统一协议最终数字（ign_f1，可写入论文）**:\\n  - SFT baseline: ign_f1=0.3974\\n  - CED RSFT mean: ign_f1=0.4467 (s42=0.4423, s44=0.4511)\\n  - flat-NLI RSFT mean: ign_f1=0.4475 (s42=0.4448, s43_r2=0.4503)\\n  - CED vs flat-NLI Δ=0.0008，统计不可区分（D078 p=0.44）\\n- **Cross-family validation (D082/D130)**: LLaMA-3.1-8B SFT ign_f1=0.5384 (rel_f1=0.5514), evi_f1=0.8151; no-evidence ign_f1=0.5452 (rel_f1=0.5624). SFT evidence tax=+0.68pp（微弱代价）\\n- **Cross-dataset validation (D080)**: Re-DocRED Ign-F1 下降 3.15pp 但 Evi-F1 保持 0.817——evidence extraction 能力跨数据集泛化\\n- 需要 DREEAM-protocol 对齐的 Evi-F1 对比（D041 在处理中）\\n- **RSFT 方法论定位**：诚实承认 RSFT 是 STaR (NeurIPS 2022) 的单轮变体 + evidence quality scoring。创新点在于将 STaR-like self-improvement 应用于 evidence-grounded DocRE + CED/NLI 作为 domain-specific quality filter，而非框架本身\\n- **Evaluation caveat** (Limitations): GenRES 指出 exact match F1 低估 generative RE，FREIGE 所有 eval 用 exact match，应承认此局限（对所有方法同等适用，相对比较有效）\\n\\n### Claim 2 (Core finding): RL optimization pressure 与 evidence-extraction decoupling severity 正相关（D068/D069 修正）\\n- **Decoupling spectrum**: 优化强度越大，decoupling 越严重\\n  - GRPO QLoRA: catastrophic（rel_f1=0.0138, evi_f1=0.850, EDCR=0.99）— QLoRA 放大 decoupling\\n  - GRPO bf16: mild（rel_f1 从 0.4549→~0.434, -2pp; step_30=0.434, step_50=0.4276, step_70=0.4341）— 退化可控但持续（D065/D066）\\n  - DPO bf16 1ep→3ep: gradual（Ign-F1 38.88→TBD, rel_f1 0.4034→0.3429）— 随训练渐进退化（D067/D068）\\n  - RSFT: none — 无 decoupling，唯一有效路线\\n- **QLoRA 是 catastrophic amplifier，不是 root cause**: bf16 GRPO 仅 mild decoupling（-2pp），QLoRA 放大至 catastrophic（-39pp）（D054/D065/D066）\\n- **Decoupling 是 RL-for-IE 的通用梯度**: 跨方法（GRPO/DPO）、跨量化策略（QLoRA/bf16）一致出现，严重程度与优化压力正相关\\n- 三档 Dream 审稿均认为此发现\\\\\\\"genuinely novel and striking\\\\\\\"\\n- **已完成**: GRPO G=8 bf16 确认 mild decoupling（plan_011 ✅）; DPO 1ep/3ep 确认 gradual decoupling（plan_012 ✅）\\n\\n### Claim 3 (Ablation): NLI-based scoring 提升 RSFT 质量——CED 与 flat-NLI 统计不可区分（D078）\\n- CED 和 flat-NLI 都显著优于 random/no scoring\\n- **D078 paired t-test 最终结论**: CED vs flat-NLI p=0.44，统计不可区分\\n  - CED RSFT mean rel_f1=0.4569 vs flat-NLI mean rel_f1=0.4561（Δ=0.0008）\\n  - 两种 scoring function 等效，CED 因可解释性（contrastive evidence discrimination）作为默认推荐\\n- ~~**Critical**: 如果 CED 不显著优于 flat-NLI → CED 降级为\\\"one possible scoring function\\\"~~ → **已确认降级（D078）**\\n- **论文叙事**: \\\"NLI-based scoring（无论 CED 或 flat-NLI）是 RSFT 有效的关键——两种变体均等效提升质量，CED 额外提供可解释的 evidence discrimination\\\"\\n- **top_k selection ablation (k=1 vs k=3)**:\\n  - k=1 (best-of-8) 优于 k=3 (top3-of-8)，CED scoring precision 随 k 增大下降\\n  - k=1 均值 (3 seeds s42/s43/s44): Ign-F1=45.00, rel_f1=0.4596, evi_f1=81.43, EDCR=0.6434\\n  - k=3 (1 seed, reeval_rsft_topk3): Ign-F1=43.64, rel_f1=0.4454, evi_f1=80.88, EDCR=0.6635\\n  - Δ (k=3 vs k=1 mean): Ign-F1 **-1.36pp**, rel_f1 **-1.42pp**, evi_f1 **-0.55pp**, EDCR +0.02\\n  - 训练数据质量下降: k=1 mean_f1=0.6157, mean_ced=0.6683 → k=3 mean_f1=0.5761, mean_ced=0.6205（来源: mvp2_rsft_ced_scoring / mvp2_rsft_ced_scoring_topk3_r2）\\n  - 训练集规模: k=1 选出 2718 样本 (12.4%), k=3 选出 8037 样本 (37%), 数据量 ×2.96 但质量下降抵消数量优势\\n  - **叙事**: 低排名 response 引入噪声——rank 2-3 样本的 F1 和 CED 分数均显著低于 rank 1，扩大训练集规模无法补偿数据质量的稀释。这表明 RSFT 的关键不在数据量而在 scoring precision\\n  - **注**: k=3 仅 1 seed，差异方向一致但尚不具备统计显著性；作为 ablation table 中的辅助行呈现，不作为独立 claim\\n  - **D102 跨 scale k=1 vs k=3 验证结果**:\\n    - 1.7B: k=1 +0.72pp ✓ | 4B: k=1 +1.02pp ✓ | LLaMA-8B: k=1 +1.51pp ✓ | 8B: k=1 -0.57pp (within seed variance 0.95pp)\\n    - **不能说 \\\"k=1 consistently outperforms k=3\\\"**（8B 方向相反）\\n    - **正确 framing**: \\\"Methodological correction to match claimed k=1 protocol yields comparable or improved results across scales\\\"\\n    - D102 的核心目标是方法学一致性修正，不是证明 k=1 更好\\n\\n### Claim 4 (Diagnostic): EDCR 双层诊断价值（D070/D072）\\n- **Layer 1 — 方法级**: EDCR 量化 evidence-extraction decoupling，与 Claim 2 spectrum 呼应\\n  - 方法级 EDCR 与 rel_f1 Spearman ρ=-0.965（plan_003 ✅）——本质是 decoupling 的度量\\n  - EDCR 识别病理模型（GRPO QLoRA: EDCR≈1.0）\\n- **Layer 2 — per-relation-type**: EDCR 提供 evi_f1 不捕捉的 relation-type 诊断信息 ✅\\n  - per-relation ρ(EDCR, evi_f1) = **-0.064** (p=0.58) → **完全独立于 evi_f1**\\n  - per-relation ρ(EDCR, rel_f1) = -0.793 (p<0.001) → 与 rel_f1 强相关但非完美\\n  - per-relation ρ(evi_f1, rel_f1) = -0.013 (p=0.91) → evidence 质量与抽取能力在 relation type 间完全解耦\\n  - 典型案例：date_of_birth (EDCR=0.118, 高 faithful) vs head_of_government (EDCR=0.956, 高 decoupled)\\n  - 93 relation types 分析（≥5 样本），76 个有非零数据\\n- **论文叙事**: EDCR 在方法级是 decoupling 度量工具（横向对比方法），在 relation 级是难度诊断工具（纵向定位薄弱 relation）——两层叙事互补\\n- **仍需**: 100 例人工标注验证（RC6）\\n\\n### Claim 5 (Scaling): Within-Family Threshold + Cross-Architecture Variation + Variance Compression（D079/D082/D083/D144/D145）\\n- **核心发现 (D144/D145, matched@2048 fair_tax)**: evidence generation 的效果存在 capacity threshold + architecture dependence\\n  - **1.7B (Qwen3-1.7B)**: fair_tax=**-5.63pp** (evidence beneficial, matched@2048 fair subset) — evidence generation 起隐式正则化作用，防止小模型过拟合\\n  - **4B (Qwen3-4B)**: fair_tax=**≈0** (3-seed: s42=+0.73, s43=-0.20, s44=+0.21; mean=+0.25±0.47pp) — D146 confirmed: 4B 容量足够 absorb evidence 负担。Base ign_f1 跨 seed std=3.6pp 但 tax std=0.47pp——tax 本身也表现出\\\"压缩\\\"特性\\n  - **8B Qwen (Qwen3-8B)**: fair_tax=**+0.04pp** (neutral, matched@2048 fair subset) — 容量完全 absorb\\n  - **8B LLaMA (LLaMA-3.1-8B)**: SFT matched@2048 fair_tax 3-seed 全负但高方差（s42=-1.53pp, s43=-11.20pp, s44=-2.83pp; mean=-5.19±5.25pp）。⚠️ 旧 @1024 mismatched 数据(s42=+1.20pp)已废弃——方向翻转是截断伪影。RSFT tax=-2.17pp±0.76pp (3-seed stable)\\n  - **14B (Qwen3-14B)**: tax=**+0.03pp** (neutral, 截断对称无需 @2048 matched)\\n- **Within-family threshold (Qwen, D146 confirmed)**: 1.7B 获益（-5.63pp，小模型需要 evidence 作为推理脚手架），4B/8B/14B 中性（4B 3-seed mean=+0.25±0.47pp, 8B +0.04pp, 14B +0.03pp）。Threshold 在 1.7B-4B 之间\\n- **Cross-architecture variation**: 同为 8B，Qwen tax≈0 vs LLaMA tax 高方差但方向一致（matched@2048: 3-seed 全负，mean=-5.19±5.25pp）。⚠️ 旧叙事\\\"direction unstable\\\"已废弃——@1024 mismatched 导致的 positive tax 是截断伪影。新叙事：LLaMA 8B evidence consistently helps（与 1.7B Qwen 方向一致），但 magnitude 极不稳定（std=5.25pp vs Qwen 4B std=0.47pp），说明 architecture/pretraining 影响 tax magnitude stability 而非 direction\\n- **⚠️ 已废弃叙事 (D144)**: \\\"non-monotonic scaling\\\"、\\\"capacity valley at 4B\\\"——M027 修正后 4B tax 从 +7.5pp→+0.73pp，低谷不存在\\n- **Mechanistic analysis (plan_020 完成)**：3 层机制解释 scale-dependent tax：\\n  - **(1) Duplication regularization（主因）**: monotonic decline — 1.7B 27.5pp gap (60.5%→33.0%), 4B 12.1pp gap (43.2%→31.1%), 8B 3.7pp gap (32.9%→29.2%)。正则化效应随容量增大递减\\n  - **(2) Truncation asymmetry**: 1.7B Δ=19pp (no-evi worse), 4B Δ=2pp (neutral), 8B Δ=-9pp (evi worse, tokens 消耗输出预算)\\n  - **(3) Fair comparison residual**: 排除截断后，1.7B evidence 仍提升 5.63pp (matched@2048 fair_tax)，8B Qwen +0.04pp，8B LLaMA seed-dependent — 证明 1.7B 有真实 reasoning scaffold 效应\\n  - **~~4B \\\"capacity valley\\\"~~ INVALIDATED (D144)**: M027 修正后 4B fair_tax=+0.73pp（非 +7.5pp），4B 不再是 outlier。plan_020 中 4B 特有的 mechanistic decomposition 需要用 matched@2048 数据重新评估\\n- **论文叙事 (D144 revised)**: \\\"Evidence as implicit regularization for small models\\\" — 推翻 \\\"evidence 总有代价\\\" 的简单叙事。1.7B 的 60.5%→33.0% duplication rate 是最有冲击力的数据点。新 framing: within-family threshold（小模型获益，大模型中性）+ cross-architecture variation + variance compression（核心）。不再是 \\\"non-monotonic curve\\\" 而是 \\\"capacity threshold + architecture dependence\\\"\\n- **Cross-family robustness (D130→LLaMA 3-seed 完成)**: \\n  - LLaMA-8B SFT 3-seed: mean=0.5109 (std=2.92pp, s42=0.5384/s43=0.5142/s44=0.4802)\\n  - LLaMA-8B RSFT 3-seed: mean=0.5654 (std=0.64pp, s42=0.5714/s43=0.5662/s44=0.5587)\\n  - RSFT gain = +5.45pp (0.5654 vs 0.5109)\\n  - **Variance compression = 4.6×** (2.92pp→0.64pp)。LLaMA 4.6× 现纳入统一范围 4.6-31×（Director D152+ 决策）\\n  - 论文叙事：跨家族 \\\"4.6-31×\\\" 统一 claim（Qwen 6.5-31× + LLaMA 4.6×）。LLaMA 作为 cross-family validation 强化 variance compression 的普遍性\\n- **Evidence 角色转化 (D130→D135 完成)**: LLaMA SFT inference-time tax = +1.35pp fair (s42, D134)。RSFT inference-time tax = -1.14pp±0.76pp (3-seed)。SFT evidence 有害 → RSFT evidence 有益——CED filtering 将 evidence 从负担转为信号。跨家族方向一致：Qwen-8B RSFT -0.78pp, LLaMA-8B RSFT -1.14pp（都是 evidence helps）\\n- **LLaMA RSFT no-evi variance compression (D135 新发现)**: RSFT no-evi std=0.13pp vs with-evi std=0.64pp (4.9× narrower)。Evidence generation 本身引入额外方差——no-evidence mode 下 RSFT 的 seed variance 更极端地被压缩。这进一步支撑 C3: variance compression 的核心驱动力是 CED filtering 消除 seed-dependent 训练噪声，evidence 生成只是次要方差源\\n- **Evidence tax methodology correction (D137: matched@2048 required)**:\\n  - **Control 结果**: s42 with-evi @2048 ign_f1=0.5649 vs @1024 ign_f1=0.5384, delta=+2.65pp。with-evi 也受 1024 截断影响。**所有 evidence tax 必须用 matched token budget（both@2048）**\\n  - **D136 \\\"方向不稳定\\\" INVALIDATED**: s42 mismatched tax=+1.20pp（positive，evidence hurts）是截断伪影。Matched@2048 下 s42 tax=-4.39pp（negative，evidence helps）。方向翻转是因为 with-evi@1024 被截断压低了\\n  - **新叙事方向**: 如果 matched@2048 下所有 seed 都是 negative → SFT evidence tax 方向一致（evidence consistently helps），但**量级不稳定**（magnitude varies across seeds）。RSFT 仍更稳定。具体 claim 待 3-seed matched 数据齐全后定\\n  - **保守性论证**: mismatched (no-evi@2048 vs with-evi@1024) 报告的 tax 是 conservative estimate——低估了 evidence 的好处。论文可用此作为 robustness argument\\n  - **RSFT truncation robustness (sub-finding 仍成立)**: RSFT no-evi truncation 18-19% at 1024，性能稳定；SFT no-evi 需要 2048 且仍有 25% 残余截断。RSFT 通过 CED filtering 学习了简洁输出结构\\n  - **待完成**: 4B s43/s44 @2048 pairs（正在跑），完成后算 3-seed 4B mean±std 确认 threshold 方向。14B RSFT @2048 不需要（截断对称）\\n  - **✅ Qwen matched@2048 已完成 (D145)**: 1.7B fair_tax=-5.63pp, 4B s42 fair_tax=+0.73pp, 8B fair_tax=+0.04pp。M027 adapter 错误同时修正。4B s43/s44 @2048 运行中\\n- **RSFT 弥合 scale gap (D094→D102→D118)**: 1.7B RSFT 3-seed mean=**0.5119** (std=0.09pp) vs 8B SFT 3-seed mean=**0.5019** (std=3.23pp)，Δ=+1.0pp, p=0.65 不显著——CED-RSFT 使 1.7B 模型**接近** 4.7× 大的 8B SFT，但差异在统计噪声内。\\\"surpasses\\\" claim 被 D118 否定（8B SFT s43=0.5324 拉高均值），回退为 \\\"approaches\\\"\\n- **Variance Compression 为核心发现 (D118, W2 修正)**: RSFT 跨 3 个尺度压缩 seed 方差——1.7B **31×** (2.79pp→0.09pp), 4B **7.5×** (3.80pp→0.51pp), 8B **6.5×** (3.23pp→0.50pp)。这不仅是副产品而是 CED filtering 的本质效应：质量过滤消除了 seed-dependent 的训练噪声。注：8B 压缩倍数从 17× 修正为 6.5×（D152: plan_006 ign_f1=0.5433 用旧 RSFT 模型 checkpoint-3393，d102 系列 k1 RSFT 正确 s42=0.5376。修正后 RSFT std=0.50pp，压缩倍数 3.23/0.50=6.5×）\\n- **14B SFT evidence tax ≈ 0 (D117)**: 14B with-evidence=0.5507, no-evidence=0.5510, tax=+0.03pp——14B 容量完全 absorb evidence 生成负担，延续 8B 的 neutral 趋势\\n- **14B RSFT 完成 (D138)**: ign_f1=**0.5886** (全项目最高), f1=0.6009, format=100%, evi_f1=0.8392, edcr=0.5396。RSFT gain=+3.79pp (vs 14B SFT 0.5507)。Evidence tax=-4.54pp（evidence 强烈有益）。**截断对称**: no-evi 10.8% vs with-evi 10.7%，@1024 tax 可信，无需 @2048 matched。RSFT 训练使 14B 输出简洁对称——与 SFT no-evi 的冗长退化形成对比\\n- **RSFT evidence tax (D119/D121)**: RSFT 系统性压缩 evidence 代价——4B RSFT tax=**+0.94pp** (@1024; SFT matched@2048 fair=+0.73pp, 两者均近零), 8B RSFT tax=**-0.78pp** (@1024)。⚠️ RSFT @1024 vs SFT @2048 matched 不直接可比——RSFT tax 也可能需 @2048 重算。但定性结论不变：RSFT 在 ≥4B 尺度 evidence tax 趋零\\n- **Test set evidence tax spot-check (D147 验证, ✅ 全部完成)**:\\n  | Scale | Dev fair_tax | Test tax | 一致? |\\n  |-------|-------------|----------|--------|\\n  | 1.7B | -5.63pp | -4.86pp | ✅ helps |\\n  | 4B | +0.25±0.47pp | -0.13pp | ✅ neutral |\\n  | 8B | +0.04pp | -1.71pp | ⚠️ dev neutral → test mild benefit |\\n  | 14B | +0.03pp | -0.32pp | ✅ neutral |\\n  | LLaMA-8B | -5.19pp | -4.94pp | ✅ helps |\\n  - **结论**: test set 上 evidence 从不 hurt performance。5/5 scales 全部与 dev 方向一致。小模型（1.7B Qwen, LLaMA-8B）获益最大\\n  - 8B deviation: 幅度小 (-1.71pp)，属 near-neutral 范围。评估协议差异（dev matched@2048 vs test full 500 docs）可能部分解释\\n  - **措辞 (Reviewer 评估)**: \\\"we observe\\\" 动词保留，可去掉 \\\"(on the development set)\\\" scope 限定改为 \\\"(within the Qwen3 family)\\\"。Mechanism-level hedge（implicit regularization）全部保留\\n- **RSFT test evidence tax (D153/D154, ✅ 全部完成)**:\\n  | Scale | RSFT with-evi | RSFT no-evi | RSFT test tax | 解读 |\\n  |-------|-------------|----------|--------|--------|\\n  | evidence dependence 的影响高度异质——1.7B/LLaMA 保持 evidence benefit, 8B near-neutral, 4B/14B 轻微 evidence cost。**Version A (\\\"systematically compresses\\\") DEAD**——2/5 scales 实际 amplify 或 flip evidence tax\\n  - **与 SFT test tax 对比**: 1.7B RSFT tax(-5.43) > SFT tax(-4.86), evidence benefit 扩大; 4B RSFT(+1.51) vs SFT(-0.13) 方向翻转; 14B RSFT(+0.77) vs SFT(-0.32) 方向翻转; 8B/LLaMA 方向一致\\n  - **叙事**: RSFT 不统一压缩 evidence tax, 而是 scale-dependent 地重塑 model-evidence 关系——小模型获益放大, 中大模型可能引入轻微代价\\n- **D112 Method B (full pipeline from s44 SFT)**: 4B RSFT bf16 ign_f1=**0.5152** (+4.18pp over SFT s44=0.4734)——确认 RSFT 不依赖特定 SFT seed，pipeline 可复现\\n\\n### Deep Error Analysis (plan_021 完成)\\n- **SFT→RSFT 三大改善机制（3 尺度一致）**:\\n  - **(1) Truncation FN 减少 -16~22%**: 1.7B 获益最大 (-841 FN)。与 evidence tax 的 truncation asymmetry 互洽\\n  - **(2) Hallucinated FP 减少 -6~12%**: LLaMA 获益最大 (-593, -11.8%)。CED filtering 淘汰了低质量 hallucinated responses\\n  - **(3) Duplicate 减少 -11~20%**: 1.7B 获益最大 (-20.2%)。与 duplication regularization 发现完全对应\\n- **未改善**: Evidence exact match 变化 <0.4pp（evidence grounding 需专门信号）; Relation confusion FP 稳定; Rare relation FN 稳定\\n- **与 Evidence Tax 分析的闭环**: Evidence regularization (tax analysis) ↔ RSFT format regularization (error analysis) 是同一机制的两个视角。小模型需要结构化约束来避免退化输出\\n- **Per-relation 获益最大**: participant, conflict, league, screenwriter, operator, contains_admin_territory — 跨句推理或长尾实体关系\\n- **Over-citation trend**: 大模型 over-citation 增加 (12%→17%)，边际预测的 evidence 精度下降\\n- **k=1 vs k=3 对比**: 1.7B + LLaMA k=1 优于 k=3; 8B k=3 略优 (+0.57 ign_f1)\\n- **关键 negative finding**: Evidence quality 完全不变 (exact match Δ<0.4pp) → RSFT 改善 RE 但不改善 evidence grounding → 需要专门的 evidence-aware 训练信号 → 直接 motivates GRPO+CED。形成完整 pipeline 故事线：SFT→RSFT(RE提升)→GRPO+CED(evidence提升)\\n- **Limitation**: \\\"FN: other\\\" 增加 ~13-15%（RSFT 消除 easy errors 但不解决 hard cases: 复杂推理、知识缺失）\\n- **产出**: art_20260518_cfce4e, LaTeX 表格已生成\\n\\n## MVP Evidence Summary (D071: 主表报 Ign-F1，附录报 F1; D076 DocRE\\\"~~\\n- **D068/D069 修正**: 重新定位为 \\\"RL optimization pressure correlates with decoupling severity\\\"——不是 GRPO 特有问题，而是 RL-for-IE 的通用梯度\\n- DPO 数据支撑 decoupling spectrum（gradual decay confirms QLoRA 直接对比\\n\\n### RC3: CED 不显著优于 flat-NLI（Dream 三档一致 Major）\\n- **已解决（D078）**: paired t-test p=0.44，CED 与 flat-NLI 统计不可区分\\n- **应对**: 不再声称 CED > flat-NLI；论文定位为 \\\"NLI-based scoring 是关键（CED 和 domain-specific quality filter\\n\\n### RC12: SAIS 说 evidence 有用，为何 FREIGE 说 evidence 有害？（文献审查 2026-05-16）\\n- **关键区分**: discriminative evidence *retrieval*（SAIS/DREEAM — 分类句子是否为证据，作为监督信号提升 RE）vs generative evidence *production*（FREIGE — LLM 生成完整证据文本，额外生成负担导致 scale-dependent 代价）\\n- SAIS 的 evidence supervision 是分类任务，不增加输出长度；FREIGE 的 evidence generation 显著增加生成序列长度，模型需分配容量同时完成 RE + evidence 生成\\n- **D079/D083/D144 新弹药**: evidence tax 存在 within-family threshold——1.7B 模型中 evidence 有益（matched@2048 fair_tax=-5.63pp），与 SAIS 的发现方向一致。4B+ 中性（非有害）。这说明 evidence 的效果取决于模型容量和任务范式的交互，而非简单的有害/有益二分\\n- **应对**: Related Work 中精准讨论此对比，明确 FREIGE 研究的是 *generative production* 设定下的代价，与 *retrieval* 设定下的增益不矛盾——两者揭示了 evidence 在不同范式下的不同角色\\n\\n### RC13: 单模型单数据集泛化性不足（D077 scope 扩展）\\n- ~~plan_006: Qwen3-1.7B / 8B model size ablation~~ ✅ D079/D083 完成\\n- ~~plan_016: LLaMA-3.1-8B cross-family validation~~ ✅ D082 完成\\n- ~~plan_007: Re-DocRED cross-dataset evaluation~~ ✅ D080 完成\\n- **结果**: 三项泛化实验全部完成，支撑 C5 scaling pattern + cross-family + cross-dataset 叙事\\n\\n### RC5: 评测可比性 — ✅ RESOLVED (D093)\\n- **DREEAM 协议确认**: DREEAM 的 evi_f1=54.01% 等价于我们的 evi_f1_joint（FP/FN triples 计入分母）\\n- 我们最好: LLaMA-8B RSFT evi_f1_joint=45.71%（差 8.3pp，主要由 RE gap 6.8pp 驱动）\\n- TP-only evi_f1=82.55% 证明正确预测 triple 的 evidence 质量优秀\\n- **论文必须**: (1) 用 evi_f1_joint 对比 DREEAM (2) 同时报 TP-only 证明 evidence 质量高 (3) 标明版本避免混淆\\n- **Framing**: generative LLM 首次做 evidence-grounded DocRE vs encoder-based 专用模型，差距主要来自 RE 而非 evidence\\n\\n### RC6: EDCR 缺人工验证\\n- 100 例小规模标注\\n\\n### RC7: Missing DPO/PPO（Dream 两档 Major）\\n- DPO 4B/LLaMA: pending\\n- **8B deviation 缓解因素**: (1) dev 用 matched@2048 format-compliant intersection protocol，test 用全量 500 docs——评估协议不同; (2) 单 seed 无 error bar; (3) -1.71pp 仍远小于 1.7B 阈值效应\\n- **措辞影响**: 保持 \\\"approximately neutral at ≥4B\\\"，不 claim \\\"exactly neutral\\\"。可加注 \\\"with possible mild benefit at larger scales\\\"\\n\\n### RC9: 连接 reward overoptimization 文献\\n- 显式引用 Gao et al. 2023\\n\\n### RC10: Post-hoc evidence retrieval baseline — ✅ RESOLVED (D091)\\n- BM25 entity_only k=2: evi_f1_joint=0.347, EDCR=0.766 → 弱于 RSFT joint generation (0.361, EDCR=0.654)\\n- Oracle_k (=GT k): evi_f1_joint=0.383 略胜 RSFT，但 EDCR 仍差 7.9pp 且不实用\\n- 防御策略：指出 joint generation 的 evidence 更 faithful（EDCR 低 11pp），且一次推理完成无需额外检索管线\\n- NLI retrieval 变体降为 rebuttal 备用\\n\\n### RC11: \\\"RL 不遗忘\\\" 文献与 Claim 2 的张力（文献扫描 2026-05-16）\\n- 近期多篇论文（arXiv 2507.05386, 2506.23508, 2509.04259）论证 RL fine-tuning 天然缓解 catastrophic forgetting\\n- **关键区分**: FREIGE 的 decoupling 是 **intra-task multi-objective trade-off**（RE 抽取 vs evidence 生成两个优化目标的竞争），不是 **inter-task forgetting**（丢失预训练知识）。两者机制不同\\n- bf16 GRPO 的 mild decoupling（-2pp）恰好印证这一点：模型没有忘记\\\"怎么做 IE\\\"，而是在 evidence reward 压力下偏移了 RE-evidence 的资源分配\\n- **应对**: Related Work / Discussion 中显式回应这组文献，区分 intra-task trade-off vs inter-task forgetting\\n- **QuRL/QaRL 文献支撑 Claim 2**: QuRL 发现 quantized vs full-precision actor divergence 在长训练中指数增长（直接支撑 QLoRA amplification 叙事）；QaRL 的 error token failure mode 与 QLoRA GRPO garbled evidence 吻合\\n\\n## Key Baselines (12+)\\n\\n1. **SFT-only**: Qwen3-4B + LoRA SFT（with evidence）\\n1b. **SFT no-evidence**: Qwen3-4B + LoRA SFT（无 evidence，upper bound ablation）\\n2. **RSFT flat-NLI**: flat-NLI scored RSFT（核心 ablation）\\n3. **RSFT CED**: CED scored RSFT（主方法）\\n4. **CED-as-reranker**: 推理时 CED QLoRA confound 排除（D065/D066）\\n8. **Post-hoc retrieval**: SFT + BM25 evidence（NEW）\\n9. **DREEAM**: encoder evidence SOTA\\n10. **SAIS**: encoder evidence baseline\\n11. **DocKS-RAG**: extraction RSFT navigates this landscape by eliminating the tax while preserving evidence faithfulness.\\\\n\\\\n本文提出 **evidence-grounded DocRE** 作为新任务范式：LLM 同时输出关系三元组和支撑证据句。核心发现是 evidence generation 的效果并非简单的有益/有害二分，而是一个由三个维度决定的 landscape：\\\\n\\\\n1. **Model capacity × evidence tax** (C3): Within-family threshold——1.7B 从 evidence 获益（matched@2048 fair_tax=-5.63pp），4B/8B/14B 趋零（+0.73pp/+0.04pp/+0.03pp）。Cross-architecture variation——LLaMA 8B 与 Qwen 8B pattern 不同（SFT tax 极不稳定，RSFT tax=-2.17pp）。⚠️ D144 修正：4B 从 +7.5pp→+0.73pp，\\\\\\\"non-monotonic\\\\\\\"/\\\\\\\"capacity valley\\\\\\\" 叙事已废弃。\\\\n2. **Training paradigm × decoupling** (C2): RL 优化压力与 evidence-extraction decoupling severity 正相关（GRPO catastrophic > DPO gradual > RSFT none）。越激进的优化越破坏 RE-evidence 平衡。\\\\n3. **Data quality × training reliability** (C1+C3): NLI-based quality filtering (CED/flat-NLI) 不仅提升 mean performance（+3-5pp），更 dramatically 压缩 seed variance（4.6-31×）。1.7B RSFT（std=0.09pp）比 8B SFT（std=3.23pp）更 predictable——小模型通过 quality filtering 获得大模型才有的稳定性。\\\\n\\\\n**RSFT 是唯一同时在三个维度上表现良好的方法**：不引入 decoupling（维度2），压缩 evidence tax 至接近零（维度1），且通过 quality filtering 保证 training reliability（维度3）。\\\\n\\\\nCED (Contrastive Evidence Discrimination) 是一种 NLI-based 候选筛选函数，与 flat-NLI 统计不可区分（D078 p=0.44），但均显著优于 random/no scoring。CED 因可解释性优势作为默认推荐。\\\\n\\\\n## Story Arc (D131: unified narrative)\\\\n\\\\n**One-sentence pitch**: Evidence generation in DocRE is a landscape shaped by capacity, paradigm, and data quality — and RSFT is the only method that navigates all three axes successfully.\\\\n\\\\n1. **Problem**: LLM DocRE 无证据 → 部署障碍\\\\n2. **New task**: evidence-grounded DocRE — 同时生成关系和证据\\\\n3. **The landscape** (统一主线的三个 facet):\\\\n   - **3a. Capacity axis** (C3): evidence tax 存在 within-family threshold——1.7B 获益（-5.63pp matched@2048 fair），4B+ 中性（+0.73pp/+0.04pp/+0.03pp）。推翻\\\\\\\"evidence 总有代价\\\\\\\"。Cross-architecture: Qwen 8B ≈0 vs LLaMA 8B evidence helpful，说明 tax 受架构影响不仅受 scale\\\\n   - **3b. Paradigm axis** (C2): RL optimization pressure 与 decoupling severity 正相关（GRPO catastrophic > DPO gradual > RSFT none）。QLoRA 放大 mild→catastrophic（intra-task trade-off，非 inter-task forgetting）\\\\n   - **3c. Quality axis** (C1): NLI-based scoring 不仅提升 mean（+3-5pp），更压缩 variance 4.6-31×（within-scale: 1.7B 31×, 4B 7.5×, 8B 6.5×, LLaMA-8B 4.6×）。Training reliability 是 quality filtering 的本质效应\\\\n4. **RSFT navigates the landscape**: 唯一同时在三个轴上表现良好的方法——无 decoupling + 压缩 tax + 保证 reliability\\\\n5. **Practical toolkit**: CED reranker（response-level，恢复 97% evidence 代价），joint generation > post-hoc retrieval（EDCR 低 11pp）\\\\n6. **Diagnostic**: EDCR 双层诊断——方法级量化 decoupling，per-relation 级定位薄弱关系类型\\\\n7. **Scaling evidence**: 4 Qwen scales (1.7B/4B/8B/14B) + LLaMA-8B 跨家族 + Re-DocRED 跨数据集\\\\n8. **Mechanistic decomposition**: duplication regularization (主因, 1.7B 最强) + truncation asymmetry + fair comparison residual — 解释 WHY 而非只报 WHAT。⚠️ 4B 特有分析需 @2048 matched 数据重做（D144 invalidated \\\\\\\"capacity valley\\\\\\\"）\\\\n\\\\n## Core Claims (reframed after Dream v1, updated D068-D083)\\\\n\\\\n### Claim 1 (Main): Evidence-grounded DocRE 是可行的新能力\\\\n- FREIGE 是首个**基于生成式 LLM** 的 evidence-grounded DocRE 方法（⚠️ 必须始终保持 \\\\\\\"generative LLM\\\\\\\" 限定词——SAIS 已做 encoder-based evidence retrieval + DocRE）\\\\n- **关键区分 (Reviewer warning)**：encoder-based evidence *retrieval*（SAIS, DREEAM — 分类哪些句子是证据，evidence supervision 提升 RE）vs generative evidence *production*（FREIGE — LLM 生成证据文本，额外生成负担导致 scale-dependent 代价）。论文 Related Work 必须精准讨论此对比，否则审稿人会问 \\\\\\\"为什么 SAIS 说 evidence 有用，你说 evidence 有害\\\\\\\"\\\\n- **Evidence generation tax**: scale-dependent，详见 Claim 5（D079/D082/D083）\\\\n- RSFT 在 4/4 Qwen 配置 + LLaMA 下均超过 SFT baseline（Ign-F1 +1.24~6.55pp）\\\\n- **CED reranker 是最优实用方案**: rel_f1=0.4864（仅低于 no-evidence 0.29pp），evi_f1=0.8188（plan_005 ✅）\\\\n- **D076 统一协议最终数字（ign_f1，可写入论文）**:\\\\n  - SFT baseline: ign_f1=0.3974\\\\n  - CED RSFT mean: ign_f1=0.4467 (s42=0.4423, s44=0.4511)\\\\n  - flat-NLI RSFT mean: ign_f1=0.4475 (s42=0.4448, s43_r2=0.4503)\\\\n  - CED vs flat-NLI Δ=0.0008，统计不可区分（D078 p=0.44）\\\\n- **Cross-family validation (D082/D130)**: LLaMA-3.1-8B SFT ign_f1=0.5384 (rel_f1=0.5514), evi_f1=0.8151; no-evidence ign_f1=0.5452 (rel_f1=0.5624). SFT evidence tax=+0.68pp（微弱代价）\\\\n- **Cross-dataset validation (D080)**: Re-DocRED Ign-F1 下降 3.15pp 但 Evi-F1 保持 0.817——evidence extraction 能力跨数据集泛化\\\\n- 需要 DREEAM-protocol 对齐的 Evi-F1 对比（D041 filter，而非框架本身\\\\n- **Evaluation caveat** (Limitations): GenRES 指出 exact match F1 低估 generative RE，FREIGE 所有 eval 用 exact match，应承认此局限（对所有方法同等适用，相对比较有效）\\\\n\\\\n### Claim 2 (Core finding): RL optimization pressure 与 evidence-extraction decoupling severity 正相关（D068/D069 修正）\\\\n- **Decoupling spectrum**: 优化强度越大，decoupling 越严重\\\\n  - GRPO QLoRA: catastrophic（rel_f1=0.0138, evi_f1=0.850, EDCR=0.99）— QLoRA 放大 decoupling\\\\n  - GRPO bf16: mild（rel_f1 从 0.4549→~0.434, -2pp; step_30=0.434, step_50=0.4276, step_70=0.4341）— 退化可控但持续（D065/D066）\\\\n  - DPO bf16 1ep→3ep: gradual（Ign-F1 38.88→TBD, rel_f1 0.4034→0.3429）— 随训练渐进退化（D067/D068）\\\\n  - RSFT: none — 无 decoupling，唯一有效路线\\\\n- **QLoRA 是 catastrophic amplifier，不是 root cause**: bf16 GRPO 仅 mild decoupling（-2pp），QLoRA 放大至 catastrophic（-39pp）（D054/D065/D066）\\\\n- **Decoupling 是 RL-for-IE 的通用梯度**: 跨方法（GRPO/DPO）、跨量化策略（QLoRA/bf16）一致出现，严重程度与优化压力正相关\\\\n- 三档 Dream 审稿均认为此发现\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"genuinely novel and striking\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\n- **已完成**: GRPO G=8 bf16 确认 mild decoupling（plan_011 ✅）; DPO 1ep/3ep 确认 gradual decoupling（plan_012 ✅）\\\\n\\\\n### Claim 3 (Ablation): NLI-based scoring 提升 RSFT 质量——CED 与 flat-NLI 统计不可区分（D078）\\\\n- CED 和 flat-NLI 都显著优于 random/no scoring\\\\n- **D078 paired t-test 最终结论**: CED vs flat-NLI p=0.44，统计不可区分\\\\n  - CED RSFT mean rel_f1=0.4569 vs flat-NLI mean rel_f1=0.4561（Δ=0.0008）\\\\n  - 两种 scoring function 等效，CED 因可解释性（contrastive evidence discrimination）作为默认推荐\\\\n- ~~**Critical**: 如果 CED 不显著优于 flat-NLI → CED 降级为\\\\\\\"one possible scoring function\\\\\\\"~~ → **已确认降级（D078）**\\\\n- **论文叙事**: \\\\\\\"NLI-based scoring（无论 CED 或 flat-NLI）是 RSFT 有效的关键——两种变体均等效提升质量，CED 额外提供可解释的 evidence discrimination\\\\\\\"\\\\n- **top_k selection ablation (k=1 vs k=3)**:\\\\n  - k=1 (best-of-8) 优于 k=3 (top3-of-8)，CED scoring precision 随 k 增大下降\\\\n  - k=1 均值 (3 seeds s42/s43/s44): Ign-F1=45.00, rel_f1=0.4596, evi_f1=81.43, EDCR=0.6434\\\\n  - k=3 (1 seed, reeval_rsft_topk3): Ign-F1=43.64, rel_f1=0.4454, evi_f1=80.88, EDCR=0.6635\\\\n  - Δ (k=3 vs k=1 mean): Ign-F1 **-1.36pp**, rel_f1 **-1.42pp**, evi_f1 **-0.55pp**, EDCR +0.02\\\\n  - 训练数据质量下降: k=1 mean_f1=0.6157, mean_ced=0.6683 → k=3 mean_f1=0.5761, mean_ced=0.6205（来源: mvp2_rsft_ced_scoring / mvp2_rsft_ced_scoring_topk3_r2）\\\\n  - 训练集规模: k=1 选出 2718 样本 (12.4%), k=3 选出 8037 样本 (37%), 数据量 ×2.96 但质量下降抵消数量优势\\\\n  - **叙事**: 低排名 response 引入噪声——rank 2-3 样本的 F1 和 CED 分数均显著低于 rank 1，扩大训练集规模无法补偿数据质量的稀释。这表明 RSFT 的关键不在数据量而在 scoring precision\\\\n  - **注**: k=3 仅 1 seed，差异方向一致但尚不具备统计显著性；作为 ablation table 中的辅助行呈现，不作为独立 claim\\\\n  - **D102 跨 scale k=1 vs k=3 验证结果**:\\\\n    - 1.7B: k=1 +0.72pp ✓ | 4B: k=1 +1.02pp ✓ | LLaMA-8B: k=1 +1.51pp ✓ | 8B: k=1 -0.57pp (within seed variance 0.95pp)\\\\n    - **不能说 \\\\\\\"k=1 consistently outperforms k=3\\\\\\\"**（8B 方向相反）\\\\n    - **正确 framing**: \\\\\\\"Methodological correction to match claimed k=1 protocol yields comparable or improved results across scales\\\\\\\"\\\\n    - D102 的核心目标是方法学一致性修正，不是证明 k=1 更好\\\\n\\\\n### Claim 4 (Diagnostic): EDCR 双层诊断价值（D070/D072）\\\\n- **Layer 1 — 方法级**: EDCR 量化 evidence-extraction decoupling，与 Claim 2 spectrum 呼应\\\\n  - 方法级 EDCR 与 rel_f1 Spearman ρ=-0.965（plan_003 ✅）——本质是 decoupling 的度量\\\\n  - EDCR 识别病理模型（GRPO QLoRA: EDCR≈1.0）\\\\n- **Layer 2 — per-relation-type**: EDCR 提供 evi_f1 不捕捉的 relation-type 诊断信息 ✅\\\\n  - per-relation ρ(EDCR, evi_f1) = **-0.064** (p=0.58) → **完全独立于 evi_f1**\\\\n  - per-relation ρ(EDCR, rel_f1) = -0.793 (p<0.001) → 与 rel_f1 强相关但非完美\\\\n  - per-relation ρ(evi_f1, rel_f1) = -0.013 (p=0.91) → evidence 质量与抽取能力在 relation type 间完全解耦\\\\n  - 典型案例：date_of_birth (EDCR=0.118, 高 faithful) vs head_of_government (EDCR=0.956, 高 decoupled)\\\\n  - 93 relation types 分析（≥5 样本），76 个有非零数据\\\\n- **论文叙事**: EDCR 在方法级是 decoupling 度量工具（横向对比方法），在 relation 级是难度诊断工具（纵向定位薄弱 relation）——两层叙事互补\\\\n- **仍需**: 100 例人工标注验证（RC6）\\\\n\\\\n### Claim 5 (Scaling): Within-Family Threshold + Cross-Architecture Variation + Variance Compression（D079/D082/D083/D144/D145）\\\\n- **核心发现 (D144/D145, matched@2048 fair_tax)**: evidence generation 的效果存在 capacity threshold + architecture dependence\\\\n  - **1.7B (Qwen3-1.7B)**: fair_tax=**-5.63pp** (evidence beneficial, matched@2048 fair subset) — evidence generation 起隐式正则化作用，防止小模型过拟合\\\\n  - **4B (Qwen3-4B)**: fair_tax=**≈0** (3-seed: s42=+0.73, s43=-0.20, s44=+0.21; mean=+0.25±0.47pp) — D146 confirmed: 4B 容量足够 absorb evidence 负担。Base ign_f1 跨 seed std=3.6pp 但 tax std=0.47pp——tax 本身也表现出\\\\\\\"压缩\\\\\\\"特性\\\\n  - **8B Qwen (Qwen3-8B)**: fair_tax=**+0.04pp** (neutral, matched@2048 fair subset) — 容量完全 absorb\\\\n  - **8B LLaMA (LLaMA-3.1-8B)**: SFT matched@2048 fair_tax 3-seed 全负但高方差（s42=-1.53pp, s43=-11.20pp, s44=-2.83pp; mean=-5.19±5.25pp）。⚠️ 旧 @1024 mismatched 数据(s42=+1.20pp)已废弃——方向翻转是截断伪影。RSFT tax=-2.17pp±0.76pp (3-seed stable)\\\\n  - **14B (Qwen3-14B)**: tax=**+0.03pp** (neutral, 截断对称无需 @2048 matched)\\\\n- **Within-family threshold (Qwen, D146 confirmed)**: 1.7B 获益（-5.63pp，小模型需要 evidence 作为推理脚手架），4B/8B/14B 中性（4B 3-seed mean=+0.25±0.47pp, 8B +0.04pp, 14B +0.03pp）。Threshold 在 1.7B-4B 之间\\\\n- **Cross-architecture variation**: 同为 8B，Qwen tax≈0 vs LLaMA tax 高方差但方向一致（matched@2048: 3-seed 全负，mean=-5.19±5.25pp）。⚠️ 旧叙事\\\\\\\"direction unstable\\\\\\\"已废弃——@1024 mismatched 导致的 positive tax 是截断伪影。新叙事：LLaMA 8B evidence consistently helps（与 1.7B Qwen 方向一致），但 magnitude 极不稳定（std=5.25pp vs Qwen 4B std=0.47pp），说明 architecture/pretraining 影响 tax magnitude stability 而非 direction\\\\n- **⚠️ 已废弃叙事 (D144)**: \\\\\\\"non-monotonic scaling\\\\\\\"、\\\\\\\"capacity valley at 4B\\\\\\\"——M027 修正后 4B tax 从 +7.5pp→+0.73pp，低谷不存在\\\\n- **Mechanistic analysis (plan_020 完成)**：3 层机制解释 scale-dependent tax：\\\\n  - **(1) Duplication regularization（主因）**: monotonic decline — 1.7B 27.5pp gap (60.5%→33.0%), 4B 12.1pp gap (43.2%→31.1%), 8B 3.7pp gap (32.9%→29.2%)。正则化效应随容量增大递减\\\\n  - **(2) Truncation asymmetry**: 1.7B Δ=19pp (no-evi worse), 4B Δ=2pp (neutral), 8B Δ=-9pp (evi worse, tokens 消耗输出预算)\\\\n  - **(3) Fair comparison residual**: 排除截断后，1.7B evidence 仍提升 5.63pp (matched@2048 fair_tax)，8B Qwen +0.04pp，8B LLaMA seed-dependent — 证明 1.7B 有真实 reasoning scaffold 效应\\\\n  - **~~4B \\\\\\\"capacity valley\\\\\\\"~~ INVALIDATED (D144)**: M027 修正后 4B fair_tax=+0.73pp（非 +7.5pp），4B 不再是 outlier。plan_020 中 4B 特有的 mechanistic decomposition 需要用 matched@2048 数据重新评估\\\\n- **论文叙事 (D144 revised)**: \\\\\\\"Evidence as implicit regularization for small models\\\\\\\" — 推翻 \\\\\\\"evidence 总有代价\\\\\\\" 的简单叙事。1.7B 的 60.5%→33.0% duplication rate 是最有冲击力的数据点。新 framing: within-family threshold（小模型获益，大模型中性）+ cross-architecture variation + variance compression（核心）。不再是 \\\\\\\"non-monotonic curve\\\\\\\" 而是 \\\\\\\"capacity threshold + architecture dependence\\\\\\\"\\\\n- **Cross-family robustness (D130→LLaMA 3-seed 完成)**: \\\\n  - LLaMA-8B SFT 3-seed: mean=0.5109 (std=2.92pp, s42=0.5384/s43=0.5142/s44=0.4802)\\\\n  - LLaMA-8B RSFT 3-seed: mean=0.5654 (std=0.64pp, s42=0.5714/s43=0.5662/s44=0.5587)\\\\n  - RSFT gain = +5.45pp (0.5654 vs 0.5109)\\\\n  - **Variance compression = 4.6×** (2.92pp→0.64pp)。LLaMA 4.6× 现纳入统一范围 4.6-31×（Director D152+ 决策）\\\\n  - 论文叙事：跨家族 \\\\\\\"4.6-31×\\\\\\\" 统一 claim（Qwen 6.5-31× + LLaMA 4.6×）。LLaMA 作为 cross-family validation 强化 variance compression 的普遍性\\\\n- **Evidence 角色转化 (D130→D135 完成)**: LLaMA SFT inference-time tax = +1.35pp fair (s42, D134)。RSFT inference-time tax = -1.14pp±0.76pp (3-seed)。SFT evidence 有害 → RSFT evidence 有益——CED filtering 将 evidence 从负担转为信号。跨家族方向一致：Qwen-8B RSFT -0.78pp, LLaMA-8B RSFT -1.14pp（都是 evidence helps）\\\\n- **LLaMA RSFT no-evi variance compression (D135 新发现)**: RSFT no-evi std=0.13pp vs with-evi std=0.64pp (4.9× narrower)。Evidence generation 本身引入额外方差——no-evidence mode 下 RSFT 的 seed variance 更极端地被压缩。这进一步支撑 C3: variance compression 的核心驱动力是 CED filtering 消除 seed-dependent 训练噪声，evidence 生成只是次要方差源\\\\n- **Evidence tax methodology correction (D137: matched@2048 required)**:\\\\n  - **Control 结果**: s42 with-evi @2048 ign_f1=0.5649 vs @1024 ign_f1=0.5384, delta=+2.65pp。with-evi 也受 1024 截断影响。**所有 evidence tax 必须用 matched token budget（both@2048）**\\\\n  - **D136 \\\\\\\"方向不稳定\\\\\\\" INVALIDATED**: s42 mismatched tax=+1.20pp（positive，evidence hurts）是截断伪影。Matched@2048 下 s42 tax=-4.39pp（negative，evidence helps）。方向翻转是因为 with-evi@1024 被截断压低了\\\\n  - **新叙事方向**: 如果 matched@2048 下所有 seed 都是 negative → SFT evidence tax 方向一致（evidence consistently helps），但**量级不稳定**（magnitude varies across seeds）。RSFT 仍更稳定。具体 claim 待 3-seed matched 数据齐全后定\\\\n  - **保守性论证**: mismatched (no-evi@2048 vs with-evi@1024) 报告的 tax 是 conservative estimate——低估了 evidence 的好处。论文可用此作为 robustness argument\\\\n  - **RSFT truncation robustness (sub-finding 仍成立)**: RSFT no-evi truncation 18-19% at 1024，性能稳定；SFT no-evi 需要 2048 且仍有 25% 残余截断。RSFT 通过 CED filtering 学习了简洁输出结构\\\\n  - **待完成**: 4B s43/s44 @2048 pairs（正在跑），完成后算 3-seed 4B mean±std 确认 threshold 方向。14B RSFT @2048 不需要（截断对称）\\\\n  - **✅ Qwen matched@2048 已完成 (D145)**: 1.7B fair_tax=-5.63pp, 4B s42 fair_tax=+0.73pp, 8B fair_tax=+0.04pp。M027 adapter 错误同时修正。4B s43/s44 @2048 运行中\\\\n- **RSFT 弥合 scale gap (D094→D102→D118)**: 1.7B RSFT 3-seed mean=**0.5119** (std=0.09pp) vs 8B SFT 3-seed mean=**0.5019** (std=3.23pp)，Δ=+1.0pp, p=0.65 不显著——CED-RSFT 使 1.7B 模型**接近** 4.7× 大的 8B SFT，但差异在统计噪声内。\\\\\\\"surpasses\\\\\\\" claim 被 D118 否定（8B SFT s43=0.5324 拉高均值），回退为 \\\\\\\"approaches\\\\\\\"\\\\n- **Variance Compression 为核心发现 (D118, W2 修正)**: RSFT 跨 3 个尺度压缩 seed 方差——1.7B **31×** (2.79pp→0.09pp), 4B **7.5×** (3.80pp→0.51pp), 8B **6.5×** (3.23pp→0.50pp)。这不仅是副产品而是 CED filtering 的本质效应：质量过滤消除了 seed-dependent 的训练噪声。注：8B 压缩倍数从 17× 修正为 6.5×（D152: plan_006 ign_f1=0.5433 用旧 RSFT 模型 checkpoint-3393，d102 系列 k1 RSFT 正确 s42=0.5376。修正后 RSFT std=0.50pp，压缩倍数 3.23/0.50=6.5×）\\\\n- **14B SFT evidence tax ≈ 0 (D117)**: 14B with-evidence=0.5507, no-evidence=0.5510, tax=+0.03pp——14B 容量完全 absorb evidence 生成负担，延续 8B 的 neutral 趋势\\\\n- **14B RSFT 完成 (D138)**: ign_f1=**0.5886** (全项目最高), f1=0.6009, format=100%, evi_f1=0.8392, edcr=0.5396。RSFT gain=+3.79pp (vs 14B SFT 0.5507)。Evidence tax=-4.54pp（evidence 强烈有益）。**截断对称**: no-evi 10.8% vs with-evi 10.7%，@1024 tax 可信，无需 @2048 matched。RSFT 训练使 14B 输出简洁对称——与 SFT no-evi 的冗长退化形成对比\\\\n- **RSFT evidence tax (D119/D121)**: RSFT 系统性压缩 evidence 代价——4B RSFT tax=**+0.94pp** (@1024; SFT matched@2048 fair=+0.73pp, 两者均近零), 8B RSFT tax=**-0.78pp** (@1024)。⚠️ RSFT @1024 vs SFT @2048 matched 不直接可比——RSFT tax 也可能需 @2048 重算。但定性结论不变：RSFT 在 ≥4B 尺度 evidence tax 趋零\\\\n- **Test set evidence tax spot-check (D147 验证, ✅ 全部完成)**:\\\\n  | Scale | Dev fair_tax | Test tax | 一致? |\\\\n  |-------|-------------|----------|--------|\\\\n  | 1.7B | -5.63pp | -4.86pp | ✅ helps |\\\\n  | 4B | +0.25±0.47pp | -0.13pp | ✅ neutral |\\\\n  | 8B | +0.04pp | -1.71pp | ⚠️ dev neutral → test mild benefit |\\\\n  | 14B | +0.03pp | -0.32pp | ✅ neutral |\\\\n  | LLaMA-8B | -5.19pp | -4.94pp | ✅ helps |\\\\n  - **结论**: test set 上 evidence 从不 hurt performance。5/5 scales 全部与 dev 方向一致。小模型（1.7B Qwen, LLaMA-8B）获益最大\\\\n  - 8B deviation: 幅度小 (-1.71pp)，属 near-neutral 范围。评估协议差异（dev matched@2048 vs test full 500 docs）可能部分解释\\\\n  - **措辞 (Reviewer 评估)**: \\\\\\\"we observe\\\\\\\" 动词保留，可去掉 \\\\\\\"(on the development set)\\\\\\\" scope 限定改为 \\\\\\\"(within the Qwen3 family)\\\\\\\"。Mechanism-level hedge（implicit Version B confirmed (\\\\\\\"heterogeneous, scale-dependent\\\\\\\")**: RSFT 对 evidence dependence 的影响高度异质——1.7B/LLaMA 保持 evidence benefit, 8B near-neutral, 4B/14B 轻微 evidence cost。**Version A (\\\\\\\"systematically compresses\\\\\\\") DEAD**——2/5 scales 实际 amplify 或 flip evidence tax\\\\n  - **与 SFT test tax 对比**: 1.7B RSFT tax(-5.43) > SFT tax(-4.86), evidence benefit 扩大; 4B RSFT(+1.51) vs SFT(-0.13) 方向翻转; 14B RSFT(+0.77) vs SFT(-0.32) 方向翻转; 8B/LLaMA 方向一致\\\\n  - **叙事**: RSFT 不统一压缩 evidence tax, 而是 scale-dependent 地重塑 model-evidence 关系——小模型获益放大, 中大模型可能引入轻微代价\\\\n- **D112 Method B (full pipeline from s44 SFT)**: 4B RSFT bf16 ign_f1=**0.5152** (+4.18pp over SFT s44=0.4734)——确认 RSFT 不依赖特定 SFT seed，pipeline 可复现\\\\n\\\\n### Deep Error Analysis (plan_021 完成)\\\\n- **SFT→RSFT 三大改善机制（3 尺度一致）**:\\\\n  - **(1) Truncation FN 减少 -16~22%**: 1.7B 获益最大 (-841 FN)。与 evidence tax 的 truncation asymmetry 互洽\\\\n  - **(2) Hallucinated FP 减少 -6~12%**: LLaMA 获益最大 (-593, -11.8%)。CED filtering 淘汰了低质量 hallucinated responses\\\\n  - **(3) Duplicate 减少 -11~20%**: 1.7B 获益最大 (-20.2%)。与 duplication regularization 发现完全对应\\\\n- **未改善**: Evidence exact match 变化 <0.4pp（evidence grounding 需专门信号）; Relation confusion FP 稳定; Rare relation FN 稳定\\\\n- **与 Evidence Tax 分析的闭环**: Evidence regularization (tax analysis) ↔ RSFT format regularization (error analysis) 是同一机制的两个视角。小模型需要结构化约束来避免退化输出\\\\n- **Per-relation 获益最大**: participant, conflict, league, screenwriter, operator, contains_admin_territory — 跨句推理或长尾实体关系\\\\n- **Over-citation trend**: 大模型 over-citation 增加 (12%→17%)，边际预测的 evidence 精度下降\\\\n- **k=1 vs k=3 对比**: 1.7B + LLaMA k=1 优于 k=3; 8B k=3 略优 (+0.57 ign_f1)\\\\n- **关键 negative finding**: Evidence quality 完全不变 (exact match Δ<0.4pp) → RSFT 改善 RE 但不改善 evidence grounding → 需要专门的 evidence-aware 训练信号 → 直接 motivates GRPO+CED。形成完整 pipeline 故事线：SFT→RSFT(RE提升)→GRPO+CED(evidence提升)\\\\n- **Limitation**: \\\\\\\"FN: other\\\\\\\" 增加 ~13-15%（RSFT 消除 easy errors 但不解决 hard cases: 复杂推理、知识缺失）\\\\n- **产出**: art_20260518_cfce4e, LaTeX 表格已生成\\\\n\\\\n## MVP Evidence Summary (D071: 主表报 Ign-F1，附录报 F1; D076 统一协议 rel_f1)\\\\n\\\\n| Config | Ign-F1 | F1 | Δ vs domain-specific quality filter\\\\n\\\\n### RC12: SAIS 说 evidence 有用，为何 FREIGE 说 evidence 有害？（文献审查 2026-05-16）\\\\n- **关键区分**: discriminative evidence *retrieval*（SAIS/DREEAM — 分类句子是否为证据，作为监督信号提升 RE）vs generative evidence *production*（FREIGE — LLM 生成完整证据文本，额外生成负担导致 scale-dependent 代价）\\\\n- SAIS 的 evidence supervision 是分类任务，不增加输出长度；FREIGE 的 evidence generation 显著增加生成序列长度，模型需分配容量同时完成 RE + evidence 生成\\\\n- **D079/D083/D144 新弹药**: evidence tax 存在 within-family threshold——1.7B 模型中 evidence 有益（matched@2048 fair_tax=-5.63pp），与 SAIS 的发现方向一致。4B+ 中性（非有害）。这说明 evidence 的效果取决于模型容量和任务范式的交互，而非简单的有害/有益二分\\\\n- **应对**: Related Work 中精准讨论此对比，明确 FREIGE 研究的是 *generative production* 设定下的代价，与 *retrieval* 设定下的增益不矛盾——两者揭示了 evidence 在不同范式下的不同角色\\\\n\\\\n### RC13: 单模型单数据集泛化性不足（D077 scope 扩展）\\\\n- ~~plan_006: Qwen3-1.7B / 8B model size ablation~~ ✅ D079/D083 完成\\\\n- ~~plan_016: LLaMA-3.1-8B cross-family validation~~ ✅ D082 完成\\\\n- ~~plan_007: Re-DocRED cross-dataset evaluation~~ ✅ D080 完成\\\\n- **结果**: 三项泛化实验全部完成，支撑 C5 scaling pattern + cross-family + cross-dataset 叙事\\\\n\\\\n### RC5: 评测可比性 — ✅ RESOLVED (D093)\\\\n- **DREEAM 协议确认**: DREEAM 的 dev 用 matched@2048 format-compliant intersection protocol，test 用全量 500 docs——评估协议不同; (2) 单 seed 无 error bar; (3) -1.71pp 仍远小于 1.7B 阈值效应\\\\n- **措辞影响**: 保持 \\\\\\\"approximately neutral at ≥4B\\\\\\\"，不 claim \\\\\\\"exactly neutral\\\\\\\"。可加注 \\\\\\\"with possible mild benefit at larger scales\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n### RC9: 连接 reward overoptimization 文献\\\\n- 显式引用 Gao et al. 2023\\\\n\\\\n### RC10: Post-hoc evidence retrieval baseline — ✅ RESOLVED (D091)\\\\n- BM25 entity_only k=2: evi_f1_joint=0.347, EDCR=0.766 → 弱于 RSFT joint generation (0.361, EDCR=0.654)\\\\n- Oracle_k (=GT k): evi_f1_joint=0.383 略胜 RSFT，但 EDCR 仍差 7.9pp 且不实用\\\\n- 防御策略：指出 joint generation 的 evidence 更 faithful（EDCR 低 11pp），且一次推理完成无需额外检索管线\\\\n- NLI retrieval 变体降为 rebuttal 备用\\\\n\\\\n### RC11: \\\\\\\"RL 不遗忘\\\\\\\" 文献与 Claim 2 的张力（文献扫描 2026-05-16）\\\\n- 近期多篇论文（arXiv 2507.05386, 2506.23508, 2509.04259）论证 RL fine-tuning 天然缓解 catastrophic forgetting\\\\n- **关键区分**: FREIGE 的 decoupling 是 **intra-task multi-objective trade-off**（RE 抽取 vs evidence 生成两个优化目标的竞争），不是 **inter-task forgetting**（丢失预训练知识）。两者机制不同\\\\n- bf16 GRPO 的 mild decoupling（-2pp）恰好印证这一点：模型没有忘记\\\\\\\"怎么做 IE\\\\\\\"，而是在 evidence reward 压力下偏移了 RE-evidence 的资源分配\\\\n- **应对**: Related Work / Discussion 中显式回应这组文献，区分 intra-task trade-off vs inter-task forgetting\\\\n- **QuRL/QaRL 文献支撑 Claim 2**: QuRL 发现 quantized vs full-precision actor divergence 在长训练中指数增长（直接支撑 QLoRA amplification 叙事）；QaRL 的 error token failure mode 与 QLoRA GRPO garbled evidence 吻合\\\\n\\\\n## Key Baselines (12+)\\\\n\\\\n1. **SFT-only**: Qwen3-4B + LoRA SFT（with evidence）\\\\n1b. **SFT no-evidence**: Qwen3-4B + LoRA SFT（无 evidence，upper bound ablation）\\\\n2. **RSFT flat-NLI**: flat-NLI scored RSFT（核心 ablation）\\\\n3. **RSFT CED**: CED scored RSFT（主方法）\\\\n4. **CED-as-reranker**: 推理时 CED 重排（无训练）\\\\n5. **DPO+CED**: offline preference learning — gradual decoupling（D067/D068）\\\\n6. **GRPO+CED (QLoRA, G=4)**: catastrophic decoupling baseline\\\\n7. **GRPO+CED (bf16, G=8)**: mild decoupling — QLoRA confound 排除（D065/D066）\\\\n8. **Post-hoc retrieval**: SFT + BM25 evidence（NEW）\\\\n9. **DREEAM**: encoder evidence SOTA\\\\n10. **SAIS**: encoder evidence baseline\\\\n11. **DocKS-RAG**: extraction SOTA（无 evidence）\\\\n12. **LMRC**: generative LLM SOTA（无 evidence）\\\\n\\\\n## Publishability Threshold (revised after D078-D083)\\\\n\\\\n- **必要条件**: (1) RSFT 在 test set 上 Ign-F1 显著 > SFT (2) DREEAM-protocol Evi-F1 可报告 (3) Decoupling spectrum 有完整数据支撑（GRPO QLoRA/bf16 + DPO + RSFT）\\\\n- **充分条件**: 上述 + EDCR 人工验证 + scaling pattern (C5) 完整\\\\n- **已达成**: CED vs flat-NLI 统计检验完成（D078: 不可区分，不再是充分条件要素）; 泛化实验完成（D079/D080/D082/D083）\\\\n- **Failure pivot**: decoupling spectrum 不成立（bf16 GRPO 也 catastrophic）→ scope 大幅缩减。**已排除**: bf16 GRPO 确认 mild（D065/D066）\\\\n\\\\n## Statistical Rigor Requirements (D131)\\\\n\\\\n- 所有 multi-seed 结果报 **sample std (ddof=1)** + **N** 标注\\\\n- 表头标注 seed 数量（e.g., \\\\\\\"mean ± std filtering)\\\\n\\\\n## Research Phase Priorities (revised D131)\\\\n\\\\n**Active（迁移后立即执行）**:\\\\n| Priority | 实验 | Claim | GPU-h | 状态 |\\\\n|----------|------|-------|-------|------|\\\\n| P0 | 14B       "result": "{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"path\": \"/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0513_6/projects/faith-evidence-ie/paper_framing.md\", \"content\": \"# Paper Framing — FREIGE\\n\\n> **状态**: 审稿修复中（D103 Action Matrix）— Round 1 得分 3.0 (Findings)，22 个 issue 跟踪中\\n> **最后更新**: 2026-05-23 (D154: RSFT test evidence tax 5/5 完成; Version B \\\"heterogeneous, scale-dependent\\\" 确认)\\n> **审稿 Round 1**: 3.0 (Findings)。3 个系统性问题：claim>evidence、实验混淆、贡献过度\\n> **贡献重构**: 4 贡献 → 3 贡献（CED 合并入 RSFT，EDCR 降级为分析工具）\\n> **Dream v1**: best=2.5, median=3.0, worst=2.5 → reframe 完成\\n> **Dream v2**: best=3.0, median=2.5, worst=2.5\\n\\n## ⚠️ Post-Review Contribution Restructuring (D103)\\n\\n审稿后从 4 贡献重构为 3 贡献：\\n\\n### 原 4 贡献\\n1. Task formalization (evidence-grounded DocRE)\\n2. EDCR diagnostic metric\\n3. Decoupling spectrum\\n4. CED scoring\\n\\n### 新 3 贡献\\n1. **Evidence-grounded DocRE + RSFT with NLI-based scoring** — 合并原 C1+C4。CED 不再作为独立贡献，NLI-based scoring（CED/flat-NLI 等效）是 RSFT 有效的关键组件\\n2. **Decoupling spectrum（观察性，非因果）** — 原 C3 降级为观察性描述。\\\"co-occurs with\\\" 替代 \\\"acts as amplifier\\\"。EDCR 作为分析工具嵌入此贡献（不独立列出）\\n3. **Evidence tax landscape + RSFT variance compression** — 原 C5 提升为正式贡献。Within-family threshold（1.7B 获益，4B+ 中性）+ cross-architecture variation（Qwen 8B ≈0 vs LLaMA 8B evidence helpful）+ RSFT 跨 scale 压缩 seed 方差 (4.6-31×, D118/D152)。**Variance compression 是 C3 核心**（最稳固的 sub-finding，不受 M027 修正影响）\\n\\n### 关键 claim 调整\\n- 所有因果 claim → 观察性描述（GRPO \\\"acts as catastrophic amplifier\\\" → \\\"co-occurs with\\\"）\\n- EDCR ρ 从 -0.965 (n=15) 改为去重后数值（待 Agent 计算完成）\\n- \\\"+8pp\\\" → matched@2048 fair subset: 1.7B=-5.63pp, 4B=+0.73pp, 8B=+0.04pp（D144 corrected）。Abstract 加 \\\"on a controlled evaluation subset\\\"\\n- \\\"matches 8B SFT\\\" → \\\"approaches 8B SFT\\\"\\n- CED vs flat-NLI 不再区分，统称 \\\"NLI-based scoring\\\"\\n\\n## Target Venue\\n\\n- Primary: **EMNLP 2026 Main** (ARR July cycle, ~July 15-25 → commit EMNLP Aug/Sep)\\n- Fallback: AAAI 2027 (abstract July 25, paper Aug 1)\\n\\n## Core Insight\\n\\n**统一主线 (D131)**: Evidence generation in LLM-based DocRE is not a fixed cost — its role depends on **model capacity** (scale-dependent tax), **training paradigm** (decoupling spectrum), and **data quality** (NLI-based filtering). RSFT navigates this landscape by eliminating the tax while preserving evidence faithfulness.\\n\\n本文提出 **evidence-grounded DocRE** 作为新任务范式：LLM 同时输出关系三元组和支撑证据句。核心发现是 evidence generation 的效果并非简单的有益/有害二分，而是一个由三个维度决定的 landscape：\\n\\n1. **Model capacity × evidence tax** (C3): Within-family threshold——1.7B 从 evidence 获益（matched@2048 fair_tax=-5.63pp），4B/8B/14B 趋零（+0.73pp/+0.04pp/+0.03pp）。Cross-architecture variation——LLaMA 8B 与 Qwen 8B pattern 不同（SFT tax 极不稳定，RSFT tax=-2.17pp）。⚠️ D144 修正：4B 从 +7.5pp→+0.73pp，\\\"non-monotonic\\\"/\\\"capacity valley\\\" 叙事已废弃。\\n2. **Training paradigm × decoupling** (C2): RL 优化压力与 evidence-extraction decoupling severity 正相关（GRPO catastrophic > DPO gradual > RSFT none）。越激进的优化越破坏 RE-evidence 平衡。\\n3. **Data quality × training reliability** (C1+C3): NLI-based quality filtering (CED/flat-NLI) 不仅提升 mean performance（+3-5pp），更 dramatically 压缩 seed variance（4.6-31×）。1.7B RSFT（std=0.09pp）比 8B SFT（std=3.23pp）更 predictable——小模型通过 quality filtering 获得大模型才有的稳定性。\\n\\n**RSFT 是唯一同时在三个维度上表现良好的方法**：不引入 decoupling（维度2），压缩 evidence tax 至接近零（维度1），且通过 quality filtering 保证 training reliability（维度3）。\\n\\nCED (Contrastive Evidence Discrimination) 是一种 NLI-based 候选筛选函数，与 flat-NLI 统计不可区分（D078 p=0.44），但均显著优于 random/no scoring。CED 因可解释性优势作为默认推荐。\\n\\n## Story Arc (D131: unified narrative)\\n\\n**One-sentence pitch**: Evidence generation in DocRE is a landscape shaped by capacity, paradigm, and data quality — and RSFT is the only method that navigates all three axes successfully.\\n\\n1. **Problem**: LLM DocRE 无证据 → 部署障碍\\n2. **New task**: evidence-grounded DocRE — 同时生成关系和证据\\n3. **The landscape** (统一主线的三个 facet):\\n   - **3a. Capacity axis** (C3): evidence tax 存在 within-family threshold——1.7B 获益（-5.63pp matched@2048 fair），4B+ 中性（+0.73pp/+0.04pp/+0.03pp）。推翻\\\"evidence 总有代价\\\"。Cross-architecture: Qwen 8B ≈0 vs LLaMA 8B evidence helpful，说明 tax 受架构影响不仅受 scale\\n   - **3b. Paradigm axis** (C2): RL optimization pressure 与 decoupling severity 正相关（GRPO catastrophic > DPO gradual > RSFT none）。QLoRA 放大 mild→catastrophic（intra-task trade-off，非 inter-task forgetting）\\n   - **3c. Quality axis** (C1): NLI-based scoring 不仅提升 mean（+3-5pp），更压缩 variance 4.6-31×（within-scale: 1.7B 31×, 4B 7.5×, 8B 6.5×, LLaMA-8B 4.6×）。Training reliability 是 quality filtering 的本质效应\\n4. **RSFT navigates the landscape**: 唯一同时在三个轴上表现良好的方法——无 decoupling + 压缩 tax + 保证 reliability\\n5. **Practical toolkit**: CED reranker（response-level，恢复 97% evidence 代价），joint generation > post-hoc retrieval（EDCR 低 11pp）\\n6. **Diagnostic**: EDCR 双层诊断——方法级量化 decoupling，per-relation 级定位薄弱关系类型\\n7. **Scaling evidence**: 4 Qwen scales (1.7B/4B/8B/14B) + LLaMA-8B 跨家族 + Re-DocRED 跨数据集\\n8. **Mechanistic decomposition**: duplication regularization (主因, 1.7B 最强) + truncation asymmetry + fair comparison residual — 解释 WHY 而非只报 WHAT。⚠️ 4B 特有分析需 @2048 matched 数据重做（D144 invalidated \\\"capacity valley\\\"）\\n\\n## Core Claims (reframed after Dream v1, updated D068-D083)\\n\\n### Claim 1 (Main): Evidence-grounded DocRE 是可行的新能力\\n- FREIGE 是首个**基于生成式 LLM** 的 evidence-grounded DocRE 方法（⚠️ 必须始终保持 \\\"generative LLM\\\" 限定词——SAIS 已做 encoder-based evidence retrieval + DocRE）\\n- **关键区分 (Reviewer warning)**：encoder-based evidence *retrieval*（SAIS, DREEAM — 分类哪些句子是证据，evidence supervision 提升 RE）vs generative evidence *production*（FREIGE — LLM 生成证据文本，额外生成负担导致 scale-dependent 代价）。论文 Related Work 必须精准讨论此对比，否则审稿人会问 \\\"为什么 SAIS 说 evidence 有用，你说 evidence 有害\\\"\\n- **Evidence generation tax**: scale-dependent，详见 Claim 5（D079/D082/D083）\\n- RSFT 在 4/4 Qwen 配置 + LLaMA 下均超过 SFT baseline（Ign-F1 +1.24~6.55pp）\\n- **CED reranker 是最优实用方案**: rel_f1=0.4864（仅低于 no-evidence 0.29pp），evi_f1=0.8188（plan_005 ✅）\\n- **D076 统一协议最终数字（ign_f1，可写入论文）**:\\n  - SFT baseline: ign_f1=0.3974\\n  - CED RSFT mean: ign_f1=0.4467 (s42=0.4423, s44=0.4511)\\n  - flat-NLI RSFT mean: ign_f1=0.4475 (s42=0.4448, s43_r2=0.4503)\\n  - CED vs flat-NLI Δ=0.0008，统计不可区分（D078 p=0.44）\\n- **Cross-family validation (D082/D130)**: LLaMA-3.1-8B SFT ign_f1=0.5384 (rel_f1=0.5514), evi_f1=0.8151; no-evidence ign_f1=0.5452 (rel_f1=0.5624). SFT evidence tax=+0.68pp（微弱代价）\\n- **Cross-dataset validation (D080)**: Re-DocRED Ign-F1 下降 3.15pp 但 Evi-F1 保持 0.817——evidence extraction 能力跨数据集泛化\\n- 需要 DREEAM-protocol 对齐的 Evi-F1 对比（D041 在处理中）\\n- **RSFT 方法论定位**：诚实承认 RSFT 是 STaR (NeurIPS 2022) 的单轮变体 + evidence quality scoring。创新点在于将 STaR-like self-improvement 应用于 evidence-grounded DocRE + CED/NLI 作为 domain-specific quality filter，而非框架本身\\n- **Evaluation caveat** (Limitations): GenRES 指出 exact match F1 低估 generative RE，FREIGE 所有 eval 用 exact match，应承认此局限（对所有方法同等适用，相对比较有效）\\n\\n### Claim 2 (Core finding): RL optimization pressure 与 evidence-extraction decoupling severity 正相关（D068/D069 修正）\\n- **Decoupling spectrum**: 优化强度越大，decoupling 越严重\\n  - GRPO QLoRA: catastrophic（rel_f1=0.0138, evi_f1=0.850, EDCR=0.99）— QLoRA 放大 decoupling\\n  - GRPO bf16: mild（rel_f1 从 0.4549→~0.434, -2pp; step_30=0.434, step_50=0.4276, step_70=0.4341）— 退化可控但持续（D065/D066）\\n  - DPO bf16 1ep→3ep: gradual（Ign-F1 38.88→TBD, rel_f1 0.4034→0.3429）— 随训练渐进退化（D067/D068）\\n  - RSFT: none — 无 decoupling，唯一有效路线\\n- **QLoRA 是 catastrophic amplifier，不是 root cause**: bf16 GRPO 仅 mild decoupling（-2pp），QLoRA 放大至 catastrophic（-39pp）（D054/D065/D066）\\n- **Decoupling 是 RL-for-IE 的通用梯度**: 跨方法（GRPO/DPO）、跨量化策略（QLoRA/bf16）一致出现，严重程度与优化压力正相关\\n- 三档 Dream 审稿均认为此发现\\\\\\\"genuinely novel and striking\\\\\\\"\\n- **已完成**: GRPO G=8 bf16 确认 mild decoupling（plan_011 ✅）; DPO 1ep/3ep 确认 gradual decoupling（plan_012 ✅）\\n\\n### Claim 3 (Ablation): NLI-based scoring 提升 RSFT 质量——CED 与 flat-NLI 统计不可区分（D078）\\n- CED 和 flat-NLI 都显著优于 random/no scoring\\n- **D078 paired t-test 最终结论**: CED vs flat-NLI p=0.44，统计不可区分\\n  - CED RSFT mean rel_f1=0.4569 vs flat-NLI mean rel_f1=0.4561（Δ=0.0008）\\n  - 两种 scoring function 等效，CED 因可解释性（contrastive evidence discrimination）作为默认推荐\\n- ~~**Critical**: 如果 CED 不显著优于 flat-NLI → CED 降级为\\\"one possible scoring function\\\"~~ → **已确认降级（D078）**\\n- **论文叙事**: \\\"NLI-based scoring（无论 CED 或 flat-NLI）是 RSFT 有效的关键——两种变体均等效提升质量，CED 额外提供可解释的 evidence discrimination\\\"\\n- **top_k selection ablation (k=1 vs k=3)**:\\n  - k=1 (best-of-8) 优于 k=3 (top3-of-8)，CED scoring precision 随 k 增大下降\\n  - k=1 均值 (3 seeds s42/s43/s44): Ign-F1=45.00, rel_f1=0.4596, evi_f1=81.43, EDCR=0.6434\\n  - k=3 (1 seed, reeval_rsft_topk3): Ign-F1=43.64, rel_f1=0.4454, evi_f1=80.88, EDCR=0.6635\\n  - Δ (k=3 vs k=1 mean): Ign-F1 **-1.36pp**, rel_f1 **-1.42pp**, evi_f1 **-0.55pp**, EDCR +0.02\\n  - 训练数据质量下降: k=1 mean_f1=0.6157, mean_ced=0.6683 → k=3 mean_f1=0.5761, mean_ced=0.6205（来源: mvp2_rsft_ced_scoring / mvp2_rsft_ced_scoring_topk3_r2）\\n  - 训练集规模: k=1 选出 2718 样本 (12.4%), k=3 选出 8037 样本 (37%), 数据量 ×2.96 但质量下降抵消数量优势\\n  - **叙事**: 低排名 response 引入噪声——rank 2-3 样本的 F1 和 CED 分数均显著低于 rank 1，扩大训练集规模无法补偿数据质量的稀释。这表明 RSFT 的关键不在数据量而在 scoring precision\\n  - **注**: k=3 仅 1 seed，差异方向一致但尚不具备统计显著性；作为 ablation table 中的辅助行呈现，不作为独立 claim\\n  - **D102 跨 scale k=1 vs k=3 验证结果**:\\n    - 1.7B: k=1 +0.72pp ✓ | 4B: k=1 +1.02pp ✓ | LLaMA-8B: k=1 +1.51pp ✓ | 8B: k=1 -0.57pp (within seed variance 0.95pp)\\n    - **不能说 \\\"k=1 consistently outperforms k=3\\\"**（8B 方向相反）\\n    - **正确 framing**: \\\"Methodological correction to match claimed k=1 protocol yields comparable or improved results across scales\\\"\\n    - D102 的核心目标是方法学一致性修正，不是证明 k=1 更好\\n\\n### Claim 4 (Diagnostic): EDCR 双层诊断价值（D070/D072）\\n- **Layer 1 — 方法级**: EDCR 量化 evidence-extraction decoupling，与 Claim 2 spectrum 呼应\\n  - 方法级 EDCR 与 rel_f1 Spearman ρ=-0.965（plan_003 ✅）——本质是 decoupling 的度量\\n  - EDCR 识别病理模型（GRPO QLoRA: EDCR≈1.0）\\n- **Layer 2 — per-relation-type**: EDCR 提供 evi_f1 不捕捉的 relation-type 诊断信息 ✅\\n  - per-relation ρ(EDCR, evi_f1) = **-0.064** (p=0.58) → **完全独立于 evi_f1**\\n  - per-relation ρ(EDCR, rel_f1) = -0.793 (p<0.001) → 与 rel_f1 强相关但非完美\\n  - per-relation ρ(evi_f1, rel_f1) = -0.013 (p=0.91) → evidence 质量与抽取能力在 relation type 间完全解耦\\n  - 典型案例：date_of_birth (EDCR=0.118, 高 faithful) vs head_of_government (EDCR=0.956, 高 decoupled)\\n  - 93 relation types 分析（≥5 样本），76 个有非零数据\\n- **论文叙事**: EDCR 在方法级是 decoupling 度量工具（横向对比方法），在 relation 级是难度诊断工具（纵向定位薄弱 relation）——两层叙事互补\\n- **仍需**: 100 例人工标注验证（RC6）\\n\\n### Claim 5 (Scaling): Within-Family Threshold + Cross-Architecture Variation + Variance Compression（D079/D082/D083/D144/D145）\\n- **核心发现 (D144/D145, matched@2048 fair_tax)**: evidence generation 的效果存在 capacity threshold + architecture dependence\\n  - **1.7B (Qwen3-1.7B)**: fair_tax=**-5.63pp** (evidence beneficial, matched@2048 fair subset) — evidence generation 起隐式正则化作用，防止小模型过拟合\\n  - **4B (Qwen3-4B)**: fair_tax=**≈0** (3-seed: s42=+0.73, s43=-0.20, s44=+0.21; mean=+0.25±0.47pp) — D146 confirmed: 4B 容量足够 absorb evidence 负担。Base ign_f1 跨 seed std=3.6pp 但 tax std=0.47pp——tax 本身也表现出\\\"压缩\\\"特性\\n  - **8B Qwen (Qwen3-8B)**: fair_tax=**+0.04pp** (neutral, matched@2048 fair subset) — 容量完全 absorb\\n  - **8B LLaMA (LLaMA-3.1-8B)**: SFT matched@2048 fair_tax 3-seed 全负但高方差（s42=-1.53pp, s43=-11.20pp, s44=-2.83pp; mean=-5.19±5.25pp）。⚠️ 旧 @1024 mismatched 数据(s42=+1.20pp)已废弃——方向翻转是截断伪影。RSFT tax=-2.17pp±0.76pp (3-seed stable)\\n  - **14B (Qwen3-14B)**: tax=**+0.03pp** (neutral, 截断对称无需 @2048 matched)\\n- **Within-family threshold (Qwen, D146 confirmed)**: 1.7B 获益（-5.63pp，小模型需要 evidence 作为推理脚手架），4B/8B/14B 中性（4B 3-seed mean=+0.25±0.47pp, 8B +0.04pp, 14B +0.03pp）。Threshold 在 1.7B-4B 之间\\n- **Cross-architecture variation**: 同为 8B，Qwen tax≈0 vs LLaMA tax 高方差但方向一致（matched@2048: 3-seed 全负，mean=-5.19±5.25pp）。⚠️ 旧叙事\\\"direction unstable\\\"已废弃——@1024 mismatched 导致的 positive tax 是截断伪影。新叙事：LLaMA 8B evidence consistently helps（与 1.7B Qwen 方向一致），但 magnitude 极不稳定（std=5.25pp vs Qwen 4B std=0.47pp），说明 architecture/pretraining 影响 tax magnitude stability 而非 direction\\n- **⚠️ 已废弃叙事 (D144)**: \\\"non-monotonic scaling\\\"、\\\"capacity valley at 4B\\\"——M027 修正后 4B tax 从 +7.5pp→+0.73pp，低谷不存在\\n- **Mechanistic analysis (plan_020 完成)**：3 层机制解释 scale-dependent tax：\\n  - **(1) Duplication regularization（主因）**: monotonic decline — 1.7B 27.5pp gap (60.5%→33.0%), 4B 12.1pp gap (43.2%→31.1%), 8B 3.7pp gap (32.9%→29.2%)。正则化效应随容量增大递减\\n  - **(2) Truncation asymmetry**: 1.7B Δ=19pp (no-evi worse), 4B Δ=2pp (neutral), 8B Δ=-9pp (evi worse, tokens 消耗输出预算)\\n  - **(3) Fair comparison residual**: 排除截断后，1.7B evidence 仍提升 5.63pp (matched@2048 fair_tax)，8B Qwen +0.04pp，8B LLaMA seed-dependent — 证明 1.7B 有真实 reasoning scaffold 效应\\n  - **~~4B \\\"capacity valley\\\"~~ INVALIDATED (D144)**: M027 修正后 4B fair_tax=+0.73pp（非 +7.5pp），4B 不再是 outlier。plan_020 中 4B 特有的 mechanistic decomposition 需要用 matched@2048 数据重新评估\\n- **论文叙事 (D144 revised)**: \\\"Evidence as implicit regularization for small models\\\" — 推翻 \\\"evidence 总有代价\\\" 的简单叙事。1.7B 的 60.5%→33.0% duplication rate 是最有冲击力的数据点。新 framing: within-family threshold（小模型获益，大模型中性）+ cross-architecture variation + variance compression（核心）。不再是 \\\"non-monotonic curve\\\" 而是 \\\"capacity threshold + architecture dependence\\\"\\n- **Cross-family robustness (D130→LLaMA 3-seed 完成)**: \\n  - LLaMA-8B SFT 3-seed: mean=0.5109 (std=2.92pp, s42=0.5384/s43=0.5142/s44=0.4802)\\n  - LLaMA-8B RSFT 3-seed: mean=0.5654 (std=0.64pp, s42=0.5714/s43=0.5662/s44=0.5587)\\n  - RSFT gain = +5.45pp (0.5654 vs 0.5109)\\n  - **Variance compression = 4.6×** (2.92pp→0.64pp)。LLaMA 4.6× 现纳入统一范围 4.6-31×（Director D152+ 决策）\\n  - 论文叙事：跨家族 \\\"4.6-31×\\\" 统一 claim（Qwen 6.5-31× + LLaMA 4.6×）。LLaMA 作为 cross-family validation 强化 variance compression 的普遍性\\n- **Evidence 角色转化 (D130→D135 完成)**: LLaMA SFT inference-time tax = +1.35pp fair (s42, D134)。RSFT inference-time tax = -1.14pp±0.76pp (3-seed)。SFT evidence 有害 → RSFT evidence 有益——CED filtering 将 evidence 从负担转为信号。跨家族方向一致：Qwen-8B RSFT -0.78pp, LLaMA-8B RSFT -1.14pp（都是 evidence helps）\\n- **LLaMA RSFT no-evi variance compression (D135 新发现)**: RSFT no-evi std=0.13pp vs with-evi std=0.64pp (4.9× narrower)。Evidence generation 本身引入额外方差——no-evidence mode 下 RSFT 的 seed variance 更极端地被压缩。这进一步支撑 C3: variance compression 的核心驱动力是 CED filtering 消除 seed-dependent 训练噪声，evidence 生成只是次要方差源\\n- **Evidence tax methodology correction (D137: matched@2048 required)**:\\n  - **Control 结果**: s42 with-evi @2048 ign_f1=0.5649 vs @1024 ign_f1=0.5384, delta=+2.65pp。with-evi 也受 1024 截断影响。**所有 evidence tax 必须用 matched token budget（both@2048）**\\n  - **D136 \\\"方向不稳定\\\" INVALIDATED**: s42 mismatched tax=+1.20pp（positive，evidence hurts）是截断伪影。Matched@2048 下 s42 tax=-4.39pp（negative，evidence helps）。方向翻转是因为 with-evi@1024 被截断压低了\\n  - **新叙事方向**: 如果 matched@2048 下所有 seed 都是 negative → SFT evidence tax 方向一致（evidence consistently helps），但**量级不稳定**（magnitude varies across seeds）。RSFT 仍更稳定。具体 claim 待 3-seed matched 数据齐全后定\\n  - **保守性论证**: mismatched (no-evi@2048 vs with-evi@1024) 报告的 tax 是 conservative estimate——低估了 evidence 的好处。论文可用此作为 robustness argument\\n  - **RSFT truncation robustness (sub-finding 仍成立)**: RSFT no-evi truncation 18-19% at 1024，性能稳定；SFT no-evi 需要 2048 且仍有 25% 残余截断。RSFT 通过 CED filtering 学习了简洁输出结构\\n  - **待完成**: 4B s43/s44 @2048 pairs（正在跑），完成后算 3-seed 4B mean±std 确认 threshold 方向。14B RSFT @2048 不需要（截断对称）\\n  - **✅ Qwen matched@2048 已完成 (D145)**: 1.7B fair_tax=-5.63pp, 4B s42 fair_tax=+0.73pp, 8B fair_tax=+0.04pp。M027 adapter 错误同时修正。4B s43/s44 @2048 运行中\\n- **RSFT 弥合 scale gap (D094→D102→D118)**: 1.7B RSFT 3-seed mean=**0.5119** (std=0.09pp) vs 8B SFT 3-seed mean=**0.5019** (std=3.23pp)，Δ=+1.0pp, p=0.65 不显著——CED-RSFT 使 1.7B 模型**接近** 4.7× 大的 8B SFT，但差异在统计噪声内。\\\"surpasses\\\" claim 被 D118 否定（8B SFT s43=0.5324 拉高均值），回退为 \\\"approaches\\\"\\n- **Variance Compression 为核心发现 (D118, W2 修正)**: RSFT 跨 3 个尺度压缩 seed 方差——1.7B **31×** (2.79pp→0.09pp), 4B **7.5×** (3.80pp→0.51pp), 8B **6.5×** (3.23pp→0.50pp)。这不仅是副产品而是 CED filtering 的本质效应：质量过滤消除了 seed-dependent 的训练噪声。注：8B 压缩倍数从 17× 修正为 6.5×（D152: plan_006 ign_f1=0.5433 用旧 RSFT 模型 checkpoint-3393，d102 系列 k1 RSFT 正确 s42=0.5376。修正后 RSFT std=0.50pp，压缩倍数 3.23/0.50=6.5×）\\n- **14B SFT evidence tax ≈ 0 (D117)**: 14B with-evidence=0.5507, no-evidence=0.5510, tax=+0.03pp——14B 容量完全 absorb evidence 生成负担，延续 8B 的 neutral 趋势\\n- **14B RSFT 完成 (D138)**: ign_f1=**0.5886** (全项目最高), f1=0.6009, format=100%, evi_f1=0.8392, edcr=0.5396。RSFT gain=+3.79pp (vs 14B SFT 0.5507)。Evidence tax=-4.54pp（evidence 强烈有益）。**截断对称**: no-evi 10.8% vs with-evi 10.7%，@1024 tax 可信，无需 @2048 matched。RSFT 训练使 14B 输出简洁对称——与 SFT no-evi 的冗长退化形成对比\\n- **RSFT evidence tax (D119/D121)**: RSFT 系统性压缩 evidence 代价——4B RSFT tax=**+0.94pp** (@1024; SFT matched@2048 fair=+0.73pp, 两者均近零), 8B RSFT tax=**-0.78pp** (@1024)。⚠️ RSFT @1024 vs SFT @2048 matched 不直接可比——RSFT tax 也可能需 @2048 重算。但定性结论不变：RSFT 在 ≥4B 尺度 evidence tax 趋零\\n- **Test set evidence tax spot-check (D147 验证, ✅ 全部完成)**:\\n  | Scale | Dev fair_tax | Test tax | 一致? |\\n  |-------|-------------|----------|--------|\\n  | 1.7B | -5.63pp | -4.86pp | ✅ helps |\\n  | 4B | +0.25±0.47pp | -0.13pp | ✅ neutral |\\n  | 8B | +0.04pp | -1.71pp | ⚠️ dev neutral → test mild benefit |\\n  | 14B | +0.03pp | -0.32pp | ✅ neutral |\\n  | LLaMA-8B | -5.19pp | -4.94pp | ✅ helps |\\n  - **结论**: test set 上 evidence 从不 hurt performance。5/5 scales 全部与 dev 方向一致。小模型（1.7B Qwen, LLaMA-8B）获益最大\\n  - 8B deviation: 幅度小 (-1.71pp)，属 near-neutral 范围。评估协议差异（dev matched@2048 vs test full 500 docs）可能部分解释\\n  - **措辞 (Reviewer 评估)**: \\\"we observe\\\" 动词保留，可去掉 \\\"(on the development set)\\\" scope 限定改为 \\\"(within the Qwen3 family)\\\"。Mechanism-level hedge（implicit regularization）全部保留\\n- **RSFT test evidence tax (D153/D154, ✅ 全部完成)**:\\n  | Scale | RSFT with-evi | RSFT no-evi | RSFT test tax | 解读 |\\n  |-------|-------------|----------|--------|--------|\\n  | 1.7B | 29.33 | 23.90 | -5.43pp‡ | evidence strongly helps |\\n  | 4B | 25.01 | 26.52 | +1.51pp | only Qwen3 scale with positive RSFT tax |\\n  | 8B | 33.53 | 33.19 | -0.34pp | near-neutral |\\n  | 14B | 38.49 | 39.26 | +0.77pp† | mild evidence cost |\\n  | LLaMA-8B | 35.99 | 33.34 | -2.65pp | evidence helps |\\n  - **D153 Version B confirmed (\\\"heterogeneous, scale-dependent\\\")**: RSFT 对 evidence dependence 的影响高度异质——1.7B/LLaMA 保持 evidence benefit, 8B near-neutral, 4B/14B 轻微 evidence cost。**Version A (\\\"systematically compresses\\\") DEAD**——2/5 scales 实际 amplify 或 flip evidence 不统一压缩 evidence tax, 而是 scale-dependent 地重塑 model-evidence 关系——小模型获益放大, 中大模型可能引入轻微代价\\n- **D112 Method B (full pipeline from s44 SFT)**: 4B RSFT bf16 ign_f1=**0.5152** (+4.18pp over SFT s44=0.4734)——确认 generative evidence *production*（FREIGE — LLM 生成完整证据文本，额外生成负担导致 scale-dependent 代价）\\n- SAIS 的 evidence supervision 是分类任务，不增加输出长度；FREIGE 的 evidence generation 显著增加生成序列长度，模型需分配容量同时完成 RE + evidence 生成\\n- **D079/D083/D144 新弹药**: evidence tax 存在 within-family threshold——1.7B 模型中 evidence 有益（matched@2048 fair_tax=-5.63pp），与 SAIS 的发现方向一致。4B+ 中性（非有害）。这说明 evidence 的效果取决于模型容量和任务范式的交互，而非简单的有害/有益二分\\n- **应对**: Related Work 中精准讨论此对比，明确 FREIGE 研究的是 *generative production* 设定下的代价，与 *retrieval* 设定下的增益不矛盾——两者揭示了 evidence 在不同范式下的不同角色\\n\\n### RC13: 单模型单数据集泛化性不足（D077 scope 扩展）\\n- ~~plan_006: Qwen3-1.7B / exp_id: plan_003_edcr_diagnostic\\n    role: diagnostic_table\\n    result: EDCR vs rel_f1 Spearman=-0.965, vs evi_f1 Spearman=0.002\\n  depends_on: []\\n- id: D071\\n  date: 2026-05-15 23:50 UTC\\n  summary: EDCR 定义存在 by-construction 混淆：当前 EDCR ≈ f(1/rel_f1)，不独立度量 evidence 质量。暂停\\n    Claim 4，实现 TP-only EDCR 后重新评估\\n  rationale: plan_003 诊断表显示 EDCR vs rel_f1 Spearman r=-0.9648, EDCR vs evi_f1 r=0.0022。机理：EDCR\\n    = n_distractor/n_total 在全部预测上计算，rel_f1 低的模型预测大量错误关系→evidence 自动成为 distractor→推高\\n    EDCR。这是定义层面的混淆，非数据问题。修复方案：EDCR 改为仅在 TP triples 上计算（\\\"正确关系中 evidence 的忠实度\\\"），若 TP-only\\n    EDCR 仍与 rel_f1 强相关则 Claim 4 需大幅削弱。plan_013（人工标注验证）暂停，等 TP-only EDCR 定义确认后再决定。\\n  related_experiments:\\n  - exp_id: plan_003_edcr_diagnostic\\n    role: diagnostic\\n    result: EDCR vs rel_f1 r=-0.965, EDCR vs evi_f1 r=0.002\\n  depends_on: []\\n- id: D072\\n  date: 2026-05-16 00:00 UTC\\n  summary: 'Claim 4 升级为双层论述: 方法级 EDCR (decoupling 量化) + per-relation EDCR (独立诊断工具)'\\n  rationale: Per-relation EDCR 分析 (93 relation types) 显示 EDCR vs evi_f1 ρ=-0.064 (p=0.583)，完全独立。与方法级\\n    ρ=-0.965 形成互补。方法级 EDCR 本质是 rel_f1 的变换，诊断价值有限；但 per-relation 粒度上 EDCR 捕捉到 evi_f1\\n    看不到的信息（如 head_of_government EDCR=0.956 但 evi_f1=0.875，证据看似好但 relation 抽取极差）。同时发现\\n    evi_f1 vs rel_f1 在 per-relation 层面也近乎零相关 (ρ=-0.013)，说明 evidence quality 和 relation\\n    extraction 在不同 relation type 间完全解耦——这是一个独立的发现，强化整篇论文的 decoupling narrative。\\n  related_experiments:\\n  - exp_id: plan_003_edcr_diagnostic\\n    role: 方法级 EDCR 分析\\n    result: ρ=-0.965 vs rel_f1\\n  - exp_id: D070\\n    role: per-relation EDCR 分析\\n    result: EDCR vs evi_f1 ρ=-0.064, EDCR vs rel_f1 ρ=-0.793\\n  depends_on:\\n  - D070\\n- id: D073\\n  date: 2026-05-16 00:02 UTC\\n  summary: 'EDCR 论文定义规范: 正文用 distractor count ratio (∈[0,1])，formula proxy 1-rel_f1/evi_f1\\n    仅用于方法级快速对比；per-relation 表格须 n_pred≥10 过滤'\\n  rationale: 'Reviewer 指出两个问题: (1) EDCR formula 1-rel_f1/evi_f1 在 rel_f1>evi_f1 时为负(manufacturer=-0.1879)，名为\\n    rate 却不在[0,1]，审稿人会质疑; (2) per-relation top/bottom 表被退化 case 主导(n_pred=0→EDCR=0,\\n    TP=0→EDCR=1)，有 cherry-picking 嫌疑。解决方案: 论文正文 EDCR 定义为 distractor count ratio (cited\\n    evidence 中非支撑性证据的比例，自然∈[0,1])；formula proxy 仅在方法级无逐预测 distractor 计数时使用，论文中注明是近似。per-relation\\n    展示表格加 n_pred≥10 过滤。'\\n  related_experiments:\\n  - ```text
Now let me get the remaining data: LLaMA SFT s44 and 14B SFT evals.

Data collected so far:

**SFT ign_f1:**
- 1.7B: s42=0.47860, s43=0.42855, s44=0.43213
- 4B: s42=0.39740 (registry), s43=0.43654, s44=0.47342
- 8B: s42=0.50512, s43=0.53245, s44=0.46806
- LLaMA: s42=0.53841, s43=0.51417, s44=? (need to find)


- 1.7B RSFT: s42=0.51220 (registry), s43=0.51090, s44=0.51252
- 4B CED RSFT: s42=0.44230, s43=0.45117, s44=0.45111
- 8B RSFT: s42=0.53760, s43=0.54710, s44=0.54510
- LLaMA RSFT: s42=0.57140, s43=0.56620, s44=0.55870

Let me verify these RSFT numbers, especially the 1.7B values.

For the 1.7B model, I'm calculating the mean across the three seeds: (0.5122 + 0.5109 + 0.5125) / 3 = 0.51187, with a standard deviation around 0.00067.

Now looking at the 4B CED RSFT results across the same three seeds: the mean is 0.4482 with deviations of -0.0059, 0.0030, and 0.0029. The standard deviation comes out to approximately 0.0045, which aligns with the ~0.09pp variance noted in the paper.

Moving to the 4B SFT results, I'm seeing more variability across seeds—the mean is 0.4358 with a standard deviation of 0.0380 or about 3.80pp, which is notably higher than the CED RSFT approach. For the 8B SFT model, the mean across the three seeds is 0.5052.

I still need to track down the LLaMA SFT s44 value and pull the 14B SFT data from the paper, which mentions one seed achieving an ign_f1 of 0.5507.

{
  "command": "python3 << 'PYEOF'\nimport numpy as np\nfrom scipy import stats\n\n# === DATA (ign_f1 values from eval metrics.json) ===\n\nsft = {\n    '1.7B':     [0.47859740744194723, 0.4285507874584597, 0.43212680168698153],\n    '4B':       [0.3974, 0.43653584657945327, 0.4734224574531187],\n    '8B':       [0.5051243848480744, 0.5324471522200683, 0.46806291822368995],\n    'LLaMA-8B': [0.5384131548207386, 0.5141748600149906, 0.4802239479862742],\n}\n\nrsft = {\n    '1.7B':     [0.5122, 0.5109016739344494, 0.5125215688103344],\n    '4B':       [0.4423041303945175, 0.4511691884456671, 0.451110389911447],\n    '8B':       [0.5376, 0.5471, 0.5451076587626056],\n    'LLaMA-8B': [0.5714, 0.5662, 0.5587],\n}\n\nscales = ['1.7B', '4B', '8B', 'LLaMA-8B']\n\n# Convert to numpy\nfor s in scales:\n    sft[s] = np.array(sft[s])\n    rsft[s] = np.array(rsft[s])\n\n# ============================================================\n# 1. Sign test\n# ============================================================\nprint(\"=\" * 60)\nprint(\"## Sign test\")\nprint(\"=\" * 60)\n\nsft_stds = {}\nrsft_stds = {}\nsign_count = 0\nfor s in scales:\n    sft_std = np.std(sft[s], ddof=1) * 100  # in pp\n    rsft_std = np.std(rsft[s], ddof=1) * 100  # in pp\n    sft_stds[s] = sft_std\n    rsft_stds[s] = rsft_std\n    is_greater = sft_std > rsft_std\n    if is_greater:\n        sign_count += 1\n    print(f\"  {s:10s}: SFT_std={sft_std:.4f}pp, RSFT_std={rsft_std:.4f}pp, SFT>RSFT={is_greater}\")\n\nn_scales = len(scales)\nprint(f\"\\n{sign_count}/{n_scales} scales: SFT_std > RSFT_std\")\n\n# Binomial test (two-sided): H0: p=0.5\nbinom_result = stats.binom_test(sign_count, n_scales, 0.5)  # two-sided\n# Also compute one-sided\np_one_sided = stats.binom.sf(sign_count - 1, n_scales, 0.5)  # P(X >= sign_count)\nprint(f\"Binomial p-value (one-sided, H0: p≤0.5): {p_one_sided:.4f}\")\nprint(f\"Binomial p-value (two-sided): {binom_result:.4f}\")\n\n# ============================================================\n# 2. Chi-squared 95% CI for standard deviations\n# ============================================================\nprint(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\nprint(\"## χ² 95% CI for standard deviations\")\nprint(\"=\" * 60)\n\ndef chi2_ci_for_std(data, alpha=0.05):\n    \"\"\"Compute chi-squared CI for population std.\"\"\"\n    n = len(data)\n    s2 = np.var(data, ddof=1)\n    df = n - 1\n    chi2_lower = stats.chi2.ppf(alpha/2, df)\n    chi2_upper = stats.chi2.ppf(1 - alpha/2, df)\n    # CI for variance: [(n-1)*s^2/chi2_upper, (n-1)*s^2/chi2_lower]\n    var_lo = df * s2 / chi2_upper\n    var_hi = df * s2 / chi2_lower\n    # CI for std\n    std_lo = np.sqrt(var_lo)\n    std_hi = np.sqrt(var_hi)\n    return std_lo * 100, std_hi * 100  # in pp\n\nprint(f\"\\n{'Scale':10s} | {'n_SFT':5s} | {'SFT_std':8s} | {'SFT_std_CI':20s} | {'n_RSFT':6s} | {'RSFT_std':9s} | {'RSFT_std_CI':20s}\")\nprint(\"-\" * 90)\n\nfor s in scales:\n    n_sft = len(sft[s])\n    n_rsft = len(rsft[s])\n    sft_s = sft_stds[s]\n    rsft_s = rsft_stds[s]\n    sft_ci = chi2_ci_for_std(sft[s])\n    rsft_ci = chi2_ci_for_std(rsft[s])\n    print(f\"{s:10s} | {n_sft:5d} | {sft_s:7.4f}pp | [{sft_ci[0]:7.4f}, {sft_ci[1]:7.4f}]pp | {n_rsft:6d} | {rsft_s:8.4f}pp | [{rsft_ci[0]:7.4f}, {rsft_ci[1]:7.4f}]pp\")\n\n# Compression ratios\nprint(\"\\nVariance compression ratios:\")\nfor s in scales:\n    ratio = sft_stds[s] / rsft_stds[s]\n    print(f\"  {s:10s}: {ratio:.1f}×\")\n\n# ============================================================\n# 3. Cohen's d per scale\n# ============================================================\nprint(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\nprint(\"## Cohen's d (SFT mean vs RSFT mean)\")\nprint(\"=\" * 60)\n\ndef cohens_d(group1, group2):\n    \"\"\"Compute Cohen's d with pooled std. Positive = group2 > group1.\"\"\"\n    n1, n2 = len(group1), len(group2)\n    var1 = np.var(group1, ddof=1)\n    var2 = np.var(group2, ddof=1)\n    pooled_var = ((n1 - 1) * var1 + (n2 - 1) * var2) / (n1 + n2 - 2)\n    pooled_std = np.sqrt(pooled_var)\n    d = (np.mean(group2) - np.mean(group1)) / pooled_std\n    return d, pooled_std\n\ndef effect_label(d):\n    d_abs = abs(d)\n    if d_abs < 0.2:\n        return \"negligible\"\n    elif d_abs < 0.5:\n        return \"small\"\n    elif d_abs < 0.8:\n        return \"medium\"\n    else:\n        return \"large\"\n\nprint(f\"\\n{'Scale':10s} | {'SFT_mean':10s} | {'RSFT_mean':10s} | {'pooled_std':10s} | {'Cohen_d':9s} | {'Effect':12s}\")\nprint(\"-\" * 75)\n\nfor s in scales:\n    sft_mean = np.mean(sft[s]) * 100\n    rsft_mean = np.mean(rsft[s]) * 100\n    d, ps = cohens_d(sft[s] * 100, rsft[s] * 100)  # in pp\n    ps_pp = ps\n    label = effect_label(d)\n    print(f\"{s:10s} | {sft_mean:9.2f}pp | {rsft_mean:9.2f}pp | {ps_pp:9.4f}pp | {d:+8.4f}  | {label}\")\n\n# ============================================================\n# Summary for paper\n# ============================================================\nprint(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\nprint(\"## Paper-ready summary\")\nprint(\"=\" * 60)\n\nprint(\"\\n### Sign test\")\nprint(f\"{sign_count}/{n_scales} scales: SFT_std > RSFT_std\")\nprint(f\"Binomial p-value (one-sided): {p_one_sided:.4f}\")\nprint(f\"Note: 14B excluded (n=1 for both SFT and RSFT, std undefined)\")\n\nprint(\"\\n### χ² 95% CI for standard deviations\")\nprint(f\"| Scale     | n_SFT | SFT_std (pp) | SFT_std 95% CI (pp)   | n_RSFT | RSFT_std (pp) | RSFT_std 95% CI (pp)  | Compression |\")\nprint(f\"|-----------|-------|--------------|----------------------|--------|---------------|----------------------|-------------|\")\nfor s in scales:\n    n_sft = len(sft[s])\n    n_rsft = len(rsft[s])\n    sft_s = sft_stds[s]\n    rsft_s = rsft_stds[s]\n    sft_ci = chi2_ci_for_std(sft[s])\n    rsft_ci = chi2_ci_for_std(rsft[s])\n    ratio = sft_s / rsft_s\n    print(f\"| {s:9s} | {n_sft:5d} | {sft_s:12.2f} | [{sft_ci[0]:8.2f}, {sft_ci[1]:8.2f}] | {n_rsft:6d} | {rsft_s:13.2f} | [{rsft_ci[0]:8.2f}, {rsft_ci[1]:8.2f}] | {ratio:10.1f}× |\")\n\nprint(\"\\n### Cohen's d\")\nprint(f\"| Scale     | SFT_mean (pp) | RSFT_mean (pp) | pooled_std (pp) | Cohen's d | Effect      |\")\nprint(f\"|-----------|---------------|----------------|-----------------|-----------|-------------|\")\nfor s in scales:\n    sft_mean = np.mean(sft[s]) * 100\n    rsft_mean = np.mean(rsft[s]) * 100\n    d, ps = cohens_d(sft[s] * 100, rsft[s] * 100)\n    label = effect_label(d)\n    print(f\"| {s:9s} | {sft_mean:13.2f} | {rsft_mean:14.2f} | {ps:15.2f} | {d:+9.2f} | {label:11s} |\")\n\nPYEOF",
